<script>
var $=function(s){return document.querySelector(s)};
var E=function(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return{'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]})};
var run=function(fn){var a=[].slice.call(arguments,1);return new Promise(function(res,rej){google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res).withFailureHandler(rej)[fn].apply(null,a)})};
var MN=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'],DN=['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat'];
var items=[],imgs={},imap={},names={},allSites=[],today='',savedName='';/* savedName stays empty: nothing is remembered */
var cur={qty:{},idle:{},mach:{},today:'',week:'',check:null},edits=new Map(),iedits=new Map(),opened=false,all=null;
function msg(t){$('#msg').textContent=t;$('#msg2').textContent=t}
function tmsg(t){$('#tmsg').textContent=t}
function keep(){/* the PIN is kept only in this browser tab (sessionStorage) while signed in – see keepSave */}
function forgetOld(){try{['wtc_name','wtc_site','wtc_pin'].forEach(function(k){localStorage.removeItem(k)})}catch(e){}}
function empty(x){return x===''||x==null}
/* 'yyyy-mm-dd' -> '27 Oct' (no time-zone shifts) */
function fd(ds,yr){if(!ds)return'';var p=String(ds).slice(0,10).split('-');return +p[2]+' '+MN[+p[1]-1]+(yr?' '+p[0]:'')}
function fdl(ds){var p=ds.split('-'),d=new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0],+p[1]-1,+p[2]));return DN[d.getUTCDay()]+' '+fd(ds,true)}
function days(a,b){var x=a.split('-'),y=b.split('-');return(Date.UTC(+y[0],+y[1]-1,+y[2])-Date.UTC(+x[0],+x[1]-1,+x[2]))/864e5}
function sd(iso){return iso?new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString([],{day:'numeric',month:'short'}):''}
function isAll(){return $('#site').value.trim().toUpperCase()==='ALL SITES'}
var PV='PPE VIEW';function isPV(){return $('#site').value===PV}
function modeUI(){var a=isAll();$('#nameBox').hidden=a;$('#dateBox').hidden=!a;$('#open').textContent=a?'Open combined report':isX()?'Open my store':isPV()?'Open PPE (view only)':'Open my stock'}
function setNames(){
 var s=$('#site').value,l=names[s]||[],sel=$('#name'),want=sel.value||savedName;
 sel.innerHTML='<option value="">'+(!s?'Choose site first':l.length?'Choose your name…':'No names set – ask office')+'</option>'+l.map(function(n){return'<option>'+E(n)+'</option>'}).join('');
 if(l.indexOf(want)>=0)sel.value=want;
}
function closeAll(){reqs.clear();$('#mPop').hidden=true;$('#rPop').hidden=true;$('#svPop').hidden=true;rnFormClose();ORN=null;RN={list:[],types:[],cos:[]};$('#rnBadge').hidden=true;$('#mHistBox').hidden=true;mhData=null;try{skMode(false)}catch(e){}past=null;opened=false;$('#work').hidden=true;$('#bar').hidden=true;$('#office').hidden=true;$('#pdfbar').hidden=true;msg('');showTab('count');clearBasket();clearMBasket();$('#inBadge').hidden=true;xClose();nClear();aiShow(false)}
function showTab(t){try{sessionStorage.setItem('wtc_tab',t)}catch(e){}var c=t==='count',mv=MT.hasOwnProperty(t);$('#countPane').hidden=!c;$('#trPane').hidden=t!=='tr';$('#mvPane').hidden=!mv;$('#rentPane').hidden=t!=='rent';if(t==='rent'&&opened)loadRent();
 document.querySelectorAll('#acts button').forEach(function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.tab===t)});
 $('#bar').hidden=!c||!opened||isAll()||!!past;if(t==='tr'){tmsg('');loadTr()}if(mv&&opened)setMType(t)}

/* ---------- storekeeper: stock today ---------- */
function draw(){
 $('#list').innerHTML=items.map(function(it){var id=it[0],src=imgs[it[5]];
  return '<div class="it" data-id="'+E(id)+'"'+(svcKind(it[1])?' data-svk="'+svcKind(it[1])+'"':'')+' data-q="'+E((it[1]+' '+it[2]).toLowerCase())+'" data-c="'+E(it[2])+'"><input type="checkbox" class="skbox" data-sk="'+E(id)+'" aria-label="Select '+E(it[1])+' for the PDF">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<div class="nm">'+E(it[1])+'<small>'+E(it[2])+' · '+E(it[4])+'</small><small class="up"></small>'+(it[8]?'<small class="mst" hidden></small><button class="mbtn" data-mach="'+E(id)+'">🔧 Machine status</button>':'')+(svcKind(it[1])?'<small class="svl" hidden></small><button class="svh" data-svh="'+E(id)+'">History</button><button class="svb" data-svc="'+E(id)+'">'+SVK[svcKind(it[1])].ic+' '+SVK[svcKind(it[1])].btn+'</button>':'')+'</div><div class="qb"><label>Total<input inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" data-r="'+E(id)+'" aria-label="Total quantity"></label><label class="idl">Idle<input inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" data-ir="'+E(id)+'" placeholder="0" aria-label="Idle (not used) quantity"></label></div></div>'}).join('');
 var cats=[];items.forEach(function(i){if(cats.indexOf(i[2])<0)cats.push(i[2])});
 $('#cat').innerHTML='<option value="">All categories</option>'+cats.map(function(c){return'<option>'+E(c)+'</option>'}).join('');
}
function base(id){var v=cur.qty[id];return v&&!empty(v[0])?String(v[0]):''}
function baseIdle(id){var v=(cur.idle||{})[id];return v&&v[0]>0?String(v[0]):''}
function nEd(){var k=new Set();edits.forEach(function(v,i){k.add(i)});iedits.forEach(function(v,i){k.add(i)});return k.size}
function totOf(id){var v=edits.has(id)?edits.get(id):base(id);return v===''?0:Number(v)}
function machCnt(id){var v=(cur.mach||{})[id];return v?v[0]+v[1]:0}
function machTxt(v){if(!v)return'';var a=[];if(v[1]>0)a.push('🔧 '+v[1]+' at workshop'+(v[3]?' ('+v[3]+')':'')+' since '+fd(v[5]));if(v[0]>0)a.push('⚠️ '+v[0]+' needs maintenance since '+fd(v[4]));return a.join(' · ')+(v[2]?' – '+v[2]:'')}
function machRow(row,v,ro){var st=row.querySelector('.mst'),b=row.querySelector('.mbtn');row.classList.toggle('m-rp',!!v&&v[1]>0);row.classList.toggle('m-mt',!!v&&!(v[1]>0)&&v[0]>0);row.dataset.m=v?'1':'0';
 if(!st)return;st.hidden=!v;st.textContent=machTxt(v);st.classList.toggle('mt',!!v&&!(v[1]>0));b.hidden=!!ro}
function machAlert(m,ro){$('#mHistBtn').hidden=!!ro||!items.some(function(it){return it[8]&&((cur.qty||{})[it[0]]||(cur.mach||{})[it[0]])});if(ro)$('#mHistBox').hidden=true;var ks=Object.keys(m||{}),a=$('#mAlert');if(!ks.length){a.hidden=true;return}
 var w=0,mt=0;ks.forEach(function(k){w+=m[k][1];mt+=m[k][0]});
 a.hidden=false;a.innerHTML='🔧 <b>'+ks.length+' machine'+(ks.length==1?'':'s')+' need'+(ks.length==1?'s':'')+' attention</b>'+(ro?' on this day':'')+' – '+[w?w+' at workshop':'',mt?mt+' need'+(mt==1?'s':'')+' maintenance':''].filter(String).join(', ')+'. Tap to see.'}
function idleTxt(v){return v&&v[0]>0?' · '+v[0]+' idle since '+fd(v[1]):''}
/* ---------- past-day view (read only) ---------- */
var past=null;
function fillPast(){
 document.querySelectorAll('#list .it').forEach(function(row){
  var id=row.dataset.id,v=past.qty[id],d=past.day[id],i=row.querySelector('input[data-r]'),ii=row.querySelector('input[data-ir]'),pi=(past.idle||{})[id];
  i.value=v&&!empty(v[0])?v[0]:'';i.readOnly=true;row.classList.remove('ch','ich','bad');ii.value=pi?pi[0]:'';ii.readOnly=true;row.dataset.i=pi?'1':'0';
  row.dataset.t=d?'1':'0';row.dataset.e=i.value===''?'1':'0';
  row.querySelector('.up').textContent=(!v?'No quantity yet':d?'Changed on this day: '+(empty(d[0])?'none':d[0])+' → '+(empty(d[1])?'none':d[1]):'Last changed '+fd(v[1]))+idleTxt(pi);
  machRow(row,(past.mach||{})[id],true);
 });
 machAlert(past.mach,true);svcRows(true);
 $('#today').hidden=true;$('#checkBox').hidden=true;$('#editHint').hidden=true;$('#bar').hidden=true;
 $('#pastBox').hidden=false;
 $('#pastBox').innerHTML='<b>PAST STOCK · '+E(fdl(past.date))+'</b>Stock at the end of that day. View only – nothing can be changed here. '+past.changes+' change'+(past.changes==1?'':'s')+' recorded that day.'+
  (past.check?'<br>Weekly check that week: done '+E(sd(past.check[0]))+' by '+E(past.check[1]):'')+'<button id="backToday">Back to today</button>';
 filt();
}
async function viewDay(d){
 if(!d||d===cur.today){past=null;$('#vdate').value=cur.today;fill();$('#bar').hidden=$('#countPane').hidden;msg('');return}
 if(nEd()){msg('Save your changes first (or clear them) before looking at past days.');$('#vdate').value=past?past.date:cur.today;return}
 msg('Loading '+fd(d,true)+'…');
 var r;try{r=await run('loadSiteDay',$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),d)}catch(e){msg('Connection problem. Please try again.');return}
 if(!r.ok){msg(r.error);$('#vdate').value=past?past.date:cur.today;return}
 past=r;$('#vdate').value=r.date;fill();msg('');
}
function fill(){
 if(past){fillPast();return}
 $('#pastBox').hidden=true;$('#today').hidden=false;$('#checkBox').hidden=false;$('#editHint').hidden=false;
 document.querySelectorAll('#list .it').forEach(function(row){
  var id=row.dataset.id,v=cur.qty[id],i=row.querySelector('input[data-r]'),ii=row.querySelector('input[data-ir]'),ci=(cur.idle||{})[id];i.readOnly=false;ii.readOnly=false;
  i.value=edits.has(id)?edits.get(id):base(id);row.classList.toggle('ch',edits.has(id));
  ii.value=iedits.has(id)?iedits.get(id):baseIdle(id);row.classList.toggle('ich',iedits.has(id));row.dataset.i=ci?'1':'0';
  row.dataset.t=v&&v[1]===cur.today?'1':'0';row.dataset.e=base(id)===''?'1':'0';
  row.querySelector('.up').textContent=(!v?'No quantity yet':v[1]===cur.today?'Updated today'+(v[3]?' · '+v[3]:''):'Updated '+fd(v[1]))+idleTxt(ci);
  machRow(row,(cur.mach||{})[id],false);
 });
 machAlert(cur.mach,false);svcRows(false);
 $('#today').textContent='Today: '+fdl(cur.today);
 var cb=$('#checkBox');
 if(cur.check){cb.className='check done';cb.textContent='✓ Weekly check done for week of '+fd(cur.week)+' ('+sd(cur.check[0])+' by '+cur.check[1]+')'}
 else{cb.className='check';cb.innerHTML='<b>Weekly check due</b> (week of Mon '+fd(cur.week)+'). Go through every item, correct any difference, then tap:<button id="confirm">Confirm weekly check</button>'}
 filt();
}
function filt(){
 var q=$('#q').value.toLowerCase(),c=$('#cat').value,s=$('#st').value,os=$('#onlyStock').checked,n=0;
 document.querySelectorAll('#list .it').forEach(function(e){
  var ok=(!os||Number(e.querySelector('input[data-r]').value)>0)&&(!q||e.dataset.q.indexOf(q)>=0)&&(!c||e.dataset.c===c)&&(!s||(s==='c'&&(edits.has(e.dataset.id)||iedits.has(e.dataset.id)))||(s==='i'&&e.dataset.i==='1')||(s==='m'&&e.dataset.m==='1')||(s==='t'&&e.dataset.t==='1')||(s==='e'&&e.dataset.e==='1'));
  e.hidden=!ok;if(ok)n++});
 $('#cnt').textContent=n+' items';
}
function pending(){var n=nEd();msg(n?n+' change'+(n==1?'':'s')+' not saved yet':'')}
async function loadMine(quiet){
 var site=$('#site').value,pin=$('#pin').value.trim();
 if(!site||!pin){msg('Choose the site and enter the PIN.');return false}
 if(!quiet)msg('Loading…');
 var r;try{r=await run('loadSite',site,pin,$('#name').value)}catch(e){msg('Connection problem. Please try again.');return false}
 if(!r.ok){closeAll();msg(r.error);return false}
 applyState(r);if(!quiet){msg('');afterLogin()}
 loadTr();loadRent(true);if(!NL)nLoad();if(!SI)siLoad();try{lkDraw()}catch(e){}return true;
}
/* uses the fresh stock that every save sends back, so no second request is needed */
function applyState(r){
 var site=$('#site').value;
 cur=r;edits.clear();iedits.clear();opened=true;keep();past=null;$('#vdate').value=r.today;$('#vdate').max=r.today;
 $('#office').hidden=true;$('#xPane').hidden=true;$('#work').hidden=false;$('#bar').hidden=$('#countPane').hidden;if(lkMode==='store'&&lkStore()){$('#work').hidden=true;$('#bar').hidden=true;$('#xPane').hidden=false}else if(lkMode==='ppe'&&ppOn()){$('#work').hidden=true;$('#bar').hidden=true;$('#xPane').hidden=true;$('#pPane').hidden=false}fill();try{xInDraw()}catch(e){}try{wkBanDraw()}catch(e){}
 if($('#tto').dataset.for!==site){
  $('#tto').innerHTML='<option value="">Choose site…</option>'+allSites.filter(function(s){return s!==site&&s.toUpperCase()!=='ALL SITES'}).map(function(s){return'<option>'+E(s)+'</option>'}).join('');
  $('#tto').dataset.for=site;
 }
}
async function save(check){
 var name=$('#name').value;
 if(!name){msg('Choose your name first');$('#name').focus();return}
 if(!check&&!nEd()){msg('Nothing changed yet');return}
 var ids=new Set();edits.forEach(function(v,i){ids.add(i)});iedits.forEach(function(v,i){ids.add(i)});
 var bad=null,ent=[];ids.forEach(function(id){var q=edits.has(id)?edits.get(id):base(id),iv=iedits.has(id)?iedits.get(id):null;
  if(iv!==null&&iv!==''&&Number(iv)>0&&Number(iv)+machCnt(id)>(q===''?0:Number(q)))bad=id;ent.push([id,q,iv])});
 if(bad){var it=imap[bad];msg('Idle'+(machCnt(bad)?' + not working machines':'')+' cannot be more than the total for: '+(it?it[1]:bad));return}
 if(past){msg('You are looking at a past day. Tap Back to today to make changes.');return}
 var b=$('#save');b.disabled=true;msg('Saving…');showSaving();
 try{
  var r=await run('saveStock',$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),name,ent,!!check);
  if(!r.ok){hideOk();msg(r.error);b.disabled=false;return}
  keep();if(r.state)applyState(r.state);else await loadMine(true);
  var t=new Date().toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  var ic=r.idleChanged?' · '+r.idleChanged+' idle update'+(r.idleChanged==1?'':'s'):'';var okm=(check?'Weekly check confirmed ('+r.changed+' change'+(r.changed==1?'':'s')+')'+ic:!r.changed&&r.idleChanged?'Idle saved: '+r.idleChanged+' item'+(r.idleChanged==1?'':'s'):'Stock saved: '+r.changed+' change'+(r.changed==1?'':'s')+ic)+' at '+t;
  msg('✓ '+okm);bigOk(check?'Weekly check confirmed':'Stock updated',okm);
 }catch(e){hideOk();msg('Could not save. Please try again.')}
 b.disabled=false;
}
$('#list').addEventListener('input',function(e){
 var i=e.target;
 if(i.dataset.ir){var k=i.dataset.ir,w=i.value.replace(/,/g,'.').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'');if(w!==i.value)i.value=w;
  if(w.trim()===baseIdle(k)||(w.trim()===''&&baseIdle(k)===''))iedits.delete(k);else iedits.set(k,w.trim());
  var rw=i.closest('.it');rw.classList.toggle('ich',iedits.has(k));var over=w.trim()!==''&&Number(w)>0&&Number(w)+machCnt(k)>totOf(k);rw.classList.toggle('bad',over);
  if(over)msg('Idle cannot be more than the total ('+totOf(k)+(machCnt(k)?', '+machCnt(k)+' not working':'')+')');else pending();return}
 var id=i.dataset.r;if(!id)return;
 var v=i.value.replace(/,/g,'.').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'');if(v!==i.value)i.value=v;
 if(v.trim()===base(id))edits.delete(id);else edits.set(id,v.trim());
 i.closest('.it').classList.toggle('ch',edits.has(id));pending();
});
$('#checkBox').addEventListener('click',function(e){
 var b=e.target.closest('#confirm');if(!b)return;
 if(!$('#name').value){msg('Choose your name first');$('#name').focus();return}
 if(b.dataset.sure!=='1'){b.dataset.sure='1';b.textContent='I have checked every item – tap again to confirm';return}
 b.disabled=true;save(true);
});

/* ---------- transfers (one TR number can carry many items) ---------- */
var basket=[],bq={};
function tline(l,t,mode){
 var it=imap[String(l.itemId)],src=it&&imgs[it[5]],extra=l.status!=='In transit'&&l.status!=='Received'?' · <b>'+E(l.status)+'</b>':'';
 if(l.missing>0)extra+=' · '+(l.pending?'waiting for '+E(t.from)+' to check':l.shortage==='Lost'?'<b>Lost</b> (confirmed by '+E(l.shortBy)+')':'missing qty returned to '+E(t.from));
 return '<div class="tli">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<div class="nm">'+E(l.item)+'<small>'+(mode==='in'?'Sent: ':'')+E(l.qty)+' '+E(l.unit)+extra+'</small></div>'+
  (mode==='in'&&l.status==='In transit'?'<input data-rq="'+E(t.id)+'" data-item="'+E(l.itemId)+'" inputmode="decimal" value="'+E(l.qty)+'" aria-label="Quantity received">':'')+'</div>';
}
function tcard(t,mode){
 var tr=t.status==='In transit',cls=tr?'in':t.status.indexOf('Cancelled')===0?'x':t.pending?'in':'done',n=t.lines.length;
 var where=mode==='out'?'To '+E(t.to):mode==='all'?E(t.from)+' → '+E(t.to):'From '+E(t.from);
 var h='<div class="tc '+cls+'"><div class="nm">'+E(t.id)+' · '+n+' item'+(n==1?'':'s')+
  '<small>'+where+' · sent '+sd(t.sent)+' by '+E(t.by)+'</small>'+(t.note?'<small>Note: '+E(t.note)+'</small>':'')+
  '<small><b>'+E(t.status)+'</b>'+(!tr&&t.recBy?' · '+E(t.recBy)+(t.rec?' · '+sd(t.rec):''):'')+'</small>'+'<button class="txpdf" data-pdf="'+E(t.id)+'">📄 PDF</button>'+
  '<div class="tl">'+t.lines.map(function(l){return tline(l,t,mode)}).join('')+'</div>';
 if(mode==='in')h+='<p class="msg" style="margin:6px 0">Change a number only if a different quantity arrived.</p><button data-rec="'+E(t.id)+'">Received all</button>';
 return h+'</div></div>';
}
function scard(t){
 return '<div class="tc in"><div class="nm">'+E(t.id)+' · to '+E(t.to)+'<small>Received '+sd(t.rec)+' by '+E(t.recBy)+'</small><div class="tl">'+
  t.lines.filter(function(l){return l.pending}).map(function(l){var it=imap[String(l.itemId)],src=it&&imgs[it[5]];
   return '<div class="tli">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<div class="nm">'+E(l.item)+'<small>Sent '+E(l.qty)+', received '+E(l.recQty)+' → <b>'+E(l.missing)+' '+E(l.unit)+' missing</b></small>'+
    '<div class="tact"><button data-sh="here" data-t="'+E(t.id)+'" data-i="'+E(l.itemId)+'">Still here</button><button class="alt" data-sh="lost" data-t="'+E(t.id)+'" data-i="'+E(l.itemId)+'">Not here</button></div></div></div>'}).join('')+'</div></div></div>';
}
function addDays(ds,n){var p=String(ds).split('-'),d=new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0],+p[1]-1,+p[2]));d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()+n);return d.toISOString().slice(0,10)}
function defRange(f,t){var td=cur.today||today;if(!$(f).value)$(f).value=addDays(td,-30);if(!$(t).value)$(t).value=td;$(f).max=td;$(t).max=td}
function has(txt,q){return String(txt||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0}
var thData=[];
async function loadTr(){
 var site=$('#site').value,pin=$('#pin').value.trim();if(!site||!pin||isAll())return;
 defRange('#thFrom','#thTo');
 var r;try{r=await run('loadTransfers',site,pin,$('#thFrom').value,$('#thTo').value)}catch(e){tmsg('Connection problem. Please try again.');return}
 if(!r.ok){tmsg(r.error);return}
 $('#inList').innerHTML=r.incoming.length?r.incoming.map(function(t){return tcard(t,'in')}).join(''):'<p class="msg">Nothing on the way to your site.</p>';
 thData=r.history||[];
 var keep=$('#thSite').value;
 $('#thSite').innerHTML='<option value="">All sites</option>'+allSites.filter(function(s){return s!==site&&s.toUpperCase()!=='ALL SITES'}).map(function(s){return'<option>'+E(s)+'</option>'}).join('');
 $('#thSite').value=keep;drawTh();
 var sh=r.shortages||[],ns=0;sh.forEach(function(t){ns+=t.pending});
 $('#shortBox').innerHTML=ns?'<h2>Shortages to check ('+ns+')</h2><p class="msg">These items were sent from your site but arrived short. If the missing quantity is still at your site, tap <b>Still here</b> and it goes back into your stock. If not, tap <b>Not here</b> and it is marked lost for the office.</p>'+sh.map(scard).join(''):'';
 var n=r.incoming.length+ns;$('#inBadge').textContent=n;$('#inBadge').hidden=!n;
}
async function trAct(fn,args,done){
 var name=$('#name').value;if(!name){tmsg('Choose your name at the top first');$('#name').focus();return false}
 if(nEd()){tmsg('You have unsaved stock changes. Open the Stock tab and tap Save changes first.');return false}
 var bs=document.querySelectorAll('#trPane button');bs.forEach(function(b){b.disabled=true});tmsg('Saving…');showSaving();
 var r;try{r=await run.apply(null,[fn,$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),name].concat(args))}catch(e){r={ok:false,error:'Connection problem. Please try again.'}}
 bs.forEach(function(b){b.disabled=false});
 if(!r.ok){hideOk();tmsg(r.error);return false}
 if(r.state)applyState(r.state);else await loadMine(true);var okm=done(r);tmsg(okm);loadTr();bigOk(fn==='cancelTransfer'?'Transfer cancelled':fn==='receiveTransfer'?'Transfer received':fn==='resolveShortage'?'Shortage recorded':'Transfer sent',okm);return true;
}
function drawTh(){
 var me=$('#site').value,dir=$('#thDir').value,os=$('#thSite').value,st=$('#thSt').value,q=$('#thQ').value.trim().toLowerCase();
 var l=thData.filter(function(t){var out=t.from===me,other=out?t.to:t.from;
  return(!dir||(dir==='out')===out)&&(!os||other===os)&&(!st||t.status===st)&&
   (!q||has(t.id,q)||has(t.note,q)||has(t.by,q)||has(t.recBy,q)||t.lines.some(function(x){return has(x.item,q)}))});
 $('#thCnt').textContent=l.length+' transfer'+(l.length==1?'':'s')+' from '+fd($('#thFrom').value,true)+' to '+fd($('#thTo').value,true);
 $('#thList').innerHTML=l.length?l.map(function(t){return tcard(t,t.from===me?'out':'rec')}).join(''):'<p class="msg">No transfers match.</p>';
}
function ctaUpdate(){
 var a=!basket.length,b=!mbasket.length;
 $('#tq').classList.toggle('cta',a);$('#mq').classList.toggle('cta',b);
 $('#tstep').textContent=a?'👉 Step 1 · Tap the yellow box below to choose items':'Step 2 · Type the quantities, choose the site, then tap Send transfer';
 $('#mstep').textContent=b?'👉 Step 1 · Tap the yellow box below to choose items':'Step 2 · Type the quantities, fill in the details, then tap the button';
}
function drawBasket(){
 $('#bhead').hidden=!basket.length;$('#bhead').textContent='Items in this transfer ('+basket.length+')';
 $('#basket').innerHTML=basket.map(function(id){var it=imap[id],src=imgs[it[5]],h=base(String(it[0]));
  return '<div class="tli">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<div class="nm">'+E(it[1])+'<small>'+E(it[4])+' · at your site: <b>'+(h===''?'none':E(h))+'</b></small></div>'+
   '<input data-bq="'+E(id)+'" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Qty" value="'+E(bq[id]||'')+'" aria-label="Quantity to send"><button class="rm" data-brm="'+E(id)+'" aria-label="Remove">×</button></div>'}).join('');
 $('#tsend').textContent=basket.length>1?'Send transfer ('+basket.length+' items)':'Send transfer';
 ctaUpdate();
}
function pickItem(id){
 if(!imap[id])return;$('#tpick').innerHTML='';$('#tq').value='';
 if(basket.indexOf(id)<0)basket.push(id);
 drawBasket();var i=document.querySelector('[data-bq="'+id+'"]');if(i)i.focus();tmsg('');
}
function clearBasket(){basket=[];bq={};$('#tpick').innerHTML='';drawBasket();$('#tnote').value='';$('#tto').value=''}
$('#basket').addEventListener('input',function(e){var id=e.target.dataset.bq;if(!id)return;var v=e.target.value.replace(/,/g,'.').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'');if(v!==e.target.value)e.target.value=v;bq[id]=v});
$('#tsend').onclick=async function(){
 if(!basket.length){tmsg('Search and add the items to send');return}
 var lines=[];
 for(var i=0;i<basket.length;i++){var id=basket[i],q=String(bq[id]||'').trim();if(!(Number(q)>0)){tmsg('Enter the quantity for: '+imap[id][1]);document.querySelector('[data-bq="'+id+'"]').focus();return}lines.push([imap[id][0],q])}
 var to=$('#tto').value;if(!to){tmsg('Choose the site you are sending to');return}
 var ok=await trAct('sendTransfer',[to,lines,$('#tnote').value],function(r){return'Sent ✓ '+r.id+' with '+r.count+' item'+(r.count==1?'':'s')+' to '+to+'. Your stock is updated.'});
 if(ok)clearBasket();
};
$('#trPane').addEventListener('click',function(e){
 var p=e.target.closest('[data-pick]');if(p){pickItem(p.dataset.pick);return}
 var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;
 if(b.dataset.brm){basket=basket.filter(function(x){return x!==b.dataset.brm});delete bq[b.dataset.brm];drawBasket();return}
 if(b.dataset.rec){var id=b.dataset.rec,recs=[],bad='';
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-rq="'+id+'"]').forEach(function(i){var q=i.value.trim().replace(',','.');if(q===''||!(Number(q)>=0))bad=bad||i;recs.push([i.dataset.item,q])});
  if(bad){tmsg('Enter the quantity received for every item (0 if it did not arrive)');bad.focus();return}
  trAct('receiveTransfer',[id,recs],function(r){return'Received ✓ '+id+(r.diff?' – '+r.diff+' item'+(r.diff==1?'':'s')+' with a different quantity (recorded).':'')+' Your stock is updated.'})}
 if(b.dataset.sh){
  if(b.dataset.sh==='lost'&&b.dataset.sure!=='1'){b.dataset.sure='1';b.textContent='Tap again: mark lost';return}
  trAct('resolveShortage',[b.dataset.t,b.dataset.i,b.dataset.sh],function(r){return r.label==='Lost'?'Recorded: '+r.missing+' of '+r.item+' marked lost. The office can see it.':'Added back ✓ '+r.missing+' of '+r.item+' is back in your stock.'});return}
});
$('#thFrom').onchange=loadTr;$('#thTo').onchange=loadTr;
$('#thDir').onchange=drawTh;$('#thSite').onchange=drawTh;$('#thSt').onchange=drawTh;$('#thQ').oninput=drawTh;

/* ---------- stock in / out: received, used, damaged ---------- */
var MT={
 received:{label:'Received from supplier',help:'Items delivered by a supplier to your site. Your stock goes up.',i1:'Supplier',i1p:'Supplier name (required)',i2:'Delivery note / LPO no.',btn:'Record received items',need1:true},
 used:{label:'Used / consumed',help:'Consumables used on site that will not come back. Your stock goes down.',i1:'Used for (job / area)',i1p:'e.g. Villa 12, ground floor',i2:'Note',btn:'Record used items',need1:false},
 damaged:{label:'Damaged',help:'Items permanently damaged and removed from stock. Your stock goes down. 📷 A photo of every damaged item is required.',i1:'Reason / what happened',i1p:'Reason (required)',i2:'Note',btn:'Record damaged items',need1:true}
};
var mtype='received',mbasket=[],mqty={},mph={};
function mmsg(t){$('#mmsg').textContent=t}
function setMType(t){
 if(t!==mtype){mbasket=[];mqty={};mph={};$('#mi1').value='';$('#mi2').value='';$('#mq').value='';$('#mpick').innerHTML=''}
 mtype=t;var m=MT[t],cc={received:['#1b7a3a','rgba(27,122,58,.5)'],used:['#b26a00','rgba(178,106,0,.5)'],damaged:['#C42D4E','rgba(196,45,78,.5)']}[t];
 $('#mvPane').style.setProperty('--cc',cc[0]);$('#mvPane').style.setProperty('--ring',cc[1]);
 $('#mban').className='banner k-'+t;$('#mban').innerHTML='<b>'+E(m.label.toUpperCase())+'</b>'+E(m.help);
 $('#mi1L').textContent=m.i1;$('#mi1').placeholder=m.i1p;$('#mi2L').textContent=m.i2;
 $('#mhistH').textContent='History: '+m.label.toLowerCase();mmsg('');drawMBasket();loadMv();
}
function drawMBasket(){
 var m=MT[mtype];
 $('#mbhead').hidden=!mbasket.length;$('#mbhead').textContent='Items in this entry ('+mbasket.length+')';
 $('#mbasket').innerHTML=mbasket.map(function(id){var it=imap[id],src=imgs[it[5]],h=base(String(it[0]));
  return '<div class="tli">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<div class="nm">'+E(it[1])+'<small>'+E(it[4])+' · at your site: <b>'+(h===''?'none':E(h))+'</b></small></div>'+
   '<input data-mq="'+E(id)+'" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Qty" value="'+E(mqty[id]||'')+'" aria-label="Quantity"><button class="rm" data-mrm="'+E(id)+'" aria-label="Remove">×</button></div>'+(mtype==='damaged'?dmPhRow(id):'')}).join('');
 $('#msave').textContent=m.btn+(mbasket.length>1?' ('+mbasket.length+')':'');
 ctaUpdate();
}
function dmPhRow(id){var l=mph[id]||[];
 return '<div class="dmph'+(l.length?' ok':'')+'">'+l.map(function(s,i){return'<span><img alt="" src="'+s+'"><button type="button" data-dmrm="'+E(id)+'|'+i+'" aria-label="Remove photo">✕</button></span>'}).join('')+
  (l.length<DM_MAX?'<label class="dmadd">📷 '+(l.length?'Add another photo':'Add photo – required')+'<input type="file" accept="image/*" multiple data-dmf="'+E(id)+'" hidden></label>':'')+
  '<small>'+(l.length?'✔ '+l.length+' photo'+(l.length==1?'':'s'):'At least 1 photo of this damaged item')+'</small></div>'}
var DM_MAX=3;
function mcard(g,office){
 var m=MT[g.type],x=String(g.status).indexOf('Cancelled')===0,n=g.lines.length;
 var info=(g.info1?E(m.i1)+': '+E(g.info1):'')+(g.info2?(g.info1?' · ':'')+E(m.i2)+': '+E(g.info2):'');
 var h='<div class="tc '+(x?'x':g.type==='received'?'done':g.type==='damaged'?'dmg':'in')+'"><div class="nm">'+(office?E(m.label)+' · ':'')+E(g.ref)+' · '+n+' item'+(n==1?'':'s')+
  '<small>'+(office?E(g.site)+' · ':'')+fd(g.date)+' by '+E(g.by)+(x?' · <b>'+E(g.status)+'</b>':'')+'</small>'+(info?'<small>'+info+'</small>':'')+'<button class="txpdf" data-pdf="'+E(g.ref)+'">📄 PDF</button>'+
  '<div class="tl">'+g.lines.map(function(l){var it=imap[String(l.itemId)],src=it&&imgs[it[5]];
   return '<div class="tli">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<div class="nm">'+E(l.item)+'<small>'+(g.type==='received'?'+':'−')+E(l.qty)+' '+E(l.unit)+' · stock '+(l.before===''?'0':E(l.before))+' → '+E(l.after)+'</small>'+(l.ph&&l.ph.length?'<button type="button" class="dmsee" data-dmsee="'+E(l.ph.join(' '))+'" data-dmt="'+E(g.ref+' · '+l.item)+'">📷 '+l.ph.length+' photo'+(l.ph.length==1?'':'s')+'</button>':'')+'</div></div>'}).join('')+'</div>';
 return h+'</div></div>';
}
var mvData=[];
async function loadMv(){
 var site=$('#site').value,pin=$('#pin').value.trim();if(!site||!pin||isAll())return;
 defRange('#mhFrom','#mhTo');
 var t=mtype,r;try{r=await run('loadMovements',site,pin,t,$('#mhFrom').value,$('#mhTo').value)}catch(e){mmsg('Connection problem. Please try again.');return}
 if(t!==mtype)return;
 if(!r.ok){mmsg(r.error);return}
 mvData=r.list;drawMh();
}
function drawMh(){
 var q=$('#mhQ').value.trim().toLowerCase();
 var l=mvData.filter(function(g){return!q||has(g.ref,q)||has(g.info1,q)||has(g.info2,q)||has(g.by,q)||g.lines.some(function(x){return has(x.item,q)})});
 $('#mhCnt').textContent=l.length+' entr'+(l.length==1?'y':'ies')+' from '+fd($('#mhFrom').value,true)+' to '+fd($('#mhTo').value,true);
 $('#mhist').innerHTML=l.length?l.map(function(g){return mcard(g,false)}).join(''):'<p class="msg">Nothing found.</p>';
}
$('#mhFrom').onchange=loadMv;$('#mhTo').onchange=loadMv;$('#mhQ').oninput=drawMh;
async function mvAct(fn,args,done){
 var name=$('#name').value;if(!name){mmsg('Choose your name at the top first');$('#name').focus();return false}
 if(nEd()){mmsg('You have unsaved stock changes. Open the Stock tab and tap Save changes first.');return false}
 var bs=document.querySelectorAll('#mvPane button');bs.forEach(function(b){b.disabled=true});mmsg('Saving…');showSaving();
 var r;try{r=await run.apply(null,[fn,$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),name].concat(args))}catch(e){r={ok:false,error:'Connection problem. Please try again.'}}
 bs.forEach(function(b){b.disabled=false});
 if(!r.ok){hideOk();mmsg(r.error);return false}
 if(r.state)applyState(r.state);else await loadMine(true);loadMv();var okm=done(r);mmsg(okm);bigOk(fn==='cancelMovement'?'Entry cancelled':MT[args[0]]?MT[args[0]].label+' recorded':'Saved',okm);return true;
}
function clearMBasket(){mbasket=[];mqty={};mph={};$('#mpick').innerHTML='';$('#mi1').value='';$('#mi2').value='';drawMBasket()}
$('#mbasket').addEventListener('input',function(e){var id=e.target.dataset.mq;if(!id)return;var v=e.target.value.replace(/,/g,'.').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'');if(v!==e.target.value)e.target.value=v;mqty[id]=v});
$('#mbasket').addEventListener('change',async function(e){var id=e.target.dataset.dmf;if(!id)return;var fs=[].slice.call(e.target.files||[]),l=mph[id]||(mph[id]=[]),room=DM_MAX-l.length;e.target.value='';
 if(!fs.length)return;mmsg('Adding photo…');for(var i=0;i<fs.length&&i<room;i++){var d=await xImg(fs[i],1280,0.72);if(d)l.push(d)}mmsg(fs.length>room?'Up to '+DM_MAX+' photos per item.':'');drawMBasket()});
/* see the photos of a damaged entry (history / office) */
document.addEventListener('click',async function(e){var b=e.target.closest('[data-dmsee]');if(!b)return;e.preventDefault();var ids=b.dataset.dmsee.split(' ');
 $('#dPopC').innerHTML='<h4 style="margin:0 0 8px">📷 '+E(b.dataset.dmt)+'</h4><div id="dPhs">'+ids.map(function(id){return'<div class="dmbig" data-dmid="'+E(id)+'">Loading photo…</div>'}).join('')+'</div><button type="button" id="dPopX" style="width:100%;margin-top:10px">Close</button>';$('#dPop').hidden=false;
 for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){var r;try{r=await run('dmPhoto',$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),isAll()?'':$('#name').value,ids[i])}catch(x){r={ok:false,error:'Could not open the photo.'}}
  var c=document.querySelector('[data-dmid="'+ids[i]+'"]');if(c)c.innerHTML=r.ok?'<img alt="" src="'+r.src+'">':E(r.error)}});
document.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target.id==='dPopX'||e.target.id==='dPop')$('#dPop').hidden=true});
$('#msave').onclick=async function(){
 var m=MT[mtype],t=mtype;
 if(!mbasket.length){mmsg('Search and add the items first');return}
 var lines=[];
 for(var i=0;i<mbasket.length;i++){var id=mbasket[i],q=String(mqty[id]||'').trim();if(!(Number(q)>0)){mmsg('Enter the quantity for: '+imap[id][1]);document.querySelector('[data-mq="'+id+'"]').focus();return}
  if(t==='damaged'&&!(mph[id]||[]).length){mmsg('📷 Add at least 1 photo of the damaged: '+imap[id][1]);var pb=document.querySelector('[data-dmf="'+id+'"]');if(pb&&pb.parentNode)pb.parentNode.scrollIntoView({block:'center'});return}
  lines.push(t==='damaged'?[imap[id][0],q,mph[id]]:[imap[id][0],q])}
 if(m.need1&&!$('#mi1').value.trim()){mmsg('Enter the '+m.i1.toLowerCase());$('#mi1').focus();return}
 var ok=await mvAct('recordMovement',[t,lines,$('#mi1').value,$('#mi2').value],function(r){return'Recorded ✓ '+r.ref+' ('+r.count+' item'+(r.count==1?'':'s')+'). Your stock is updated.'});
 if(ok)clearMBasket();
};
$('#mvPane').addEventListener('click',function(e){
 var s=e.target.closest('[data-mt]');if(s){setMType(s.dataset.mt);return}
 var p=e.target.closest('[data-mpick]');if(p){var id=p.dataset.mpick;$('#mpick').innerHTML='';$('#mq').value='';if(mbasket.indexOf(id)<0)mbasket.push(id);drawMBasket();var i=document.querySelector('[data-mq="'+id+'"]');if(i)i.focus();mmsg('');return}
 var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;
 if(b.dataset.mrm){mbasket=mbasket.filter(function(x){return x!==b.dataset.mrm});delete mqty[b.dataset.mrm];delete mph[b.dataset.mrm];drawMBasket();return}
 if(b.dataset.dmrm){var pr=b.dataset.dmrm.split('|');(mph[pr[0]]||[]).splice(+pr[1],1);drawMBasket();return}
});

/* ---------- item picker: tap the box to see every item, type to narrow, tap to add / remove ---------- */
var PK={
 tr:{inp:'#tq',box:'#tpick',get:function(){return basket},out:function(){return true},
  toggle:function(id){var i=basket.indexOf(id);if(i>=0){basket.splice(i,1);delete bq[id]}else basket.push(id);drawBasket()}},
 mv:{inp:'#mq',box:'#mpick',get:function(){return mbasket},out:function(){return mtype!=='received'},
  toggle:function(id){var i=mbasket.indexOf(id);if(i>=0){mbasket.splice(i,1);delete mqty[id]}else mbasket.push(id);drawMBasket()}}
};
var pkOnly={tr:true,mv:true};
function openPick(k){
 var P=PK[k],box=$(P.box),old=box.querySelector('.plist'),top=old?old.scrollTop:0;
 var q=$(P.inp).value.trim().toLowerCase(),inb=P.get(),out=P.out(),only=out&&pkOnly[k];
 var list=items.filter(function(it){var id=String(it[0]),h=base(id);
  if(k==='mv'&&((mtype==='used'&&it[6]===false)||(mtype==='damaged'&&it[7]===false)))return false;/* blocked by the office on the Items tab */
  return(!q||(it[1]+' '+it[2]).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0)&&(!only||(h!==''&&Number(h)>0)||inb.indexOf(id)>=0)});
 box.innerHTML='<div class="plist"><div class="phead"><span>'+list.length+' item'+(list.length==1?'':'s')+(inb.length?' · <b>'+inb.length+' added</b>':'')+'</span>'+
  (out?'<label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" data-only="'+k+'"'+(only?' checked':'')+'> Only items at my site</label>':'')+'</div>'+
  (list.length?list.map(function(it){var id=String(it[0]),src=imgs[it[5]],h=base(id),on=inb.indexOf(id)>=0;
   return'<div class="pk'+(on?' added':'')+'" data-p="'+E(id)+'">'+(src?'<img alt="" loading="lazy" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<div class="nm">'+E(it[1])+'<small>'+E(it[2])+' · '+E(it[4])+' · at your site: '+(h===''?'none':'<b>'+E(h)+'</b>')+'</small></div><span class="add">'+(on?'✓ Added':'+ Add')+'</span></div>'}).join('')
   :'<p class="msg" style="padding:10px">No item found'+(only?'. Untick "Only items at my site" to see all items.':'')+'</p>')+
  '<div class="pfoot"><button data-pdone="'+k+'">Done'+(inb.length?' – enter quantities below':'')+'</button></div></div>';
 box.querySelector('.plist').scrollTop=top;
}
function closePick(k){var P=PK[k];$(P.box).innerHTML='';$(P.inp).value='';
 var b=$(k==='tr'?'#basket':'#mbasket'),e=b&&Array.prototype.filter.call(b.querySelectorAll('input'),function(i){return!i.value})[0];if(e)e.focus()}
Object.keys(PK).forEach(function(k){var P=PK[k],inp=$(P.inp),box=$(P.box);
 inp.placeholder='👆 Tap here to choose items';
 inp.addEventListener('focus',function(){openPick(k)});inp.addEventListener('click',function(){if(!$(P.box).innerHTML)openPick(k)});
 inp.addEventListener('input',function(){openPick(k)});
 box.addEventListener('click',function(e){
  var d=e.target.closest('[data-pdone]');if(d){e.stopPropagation();closePick(k);return}
  var p=e.target.closest('[data-p]');if(p){e.stopPropagation();P.toggle(p.dataset.p);openPick(k)}});
 box.addEventListener('change',function(e){if(e.target.dataset.only){pkOnly[k]=e.target.checked;openPick(k)}});
});

/* ---------- office view (site "ALL SITES") ---------- */
async function loadAll(){
 var pin=$('#pin').value.trim(),date=$('#adate').value||today;
 if(!pin){msg('Enter the office PIN.');return}
 msg('Loading all sites…');
 var r;try{r=await run('loadCombined',pin,date)}catch(e){msg('Connection problem. Please try again.');return}
 if(!r.ok){closeAll();msg(r.error);return}
 all=r;opened=true;keep();$('#work').hidden=true;$('#bar').hidden=true;$('#office').hidden=false;afterLogin();aiShow(true);
 $('#adate2').value=r.date;$('#adate2').max=r.today;
 $('#asof').textContent=(r.date===r.today?'Live stock now · ':'Stock at end of ')+fdl(r.date)+' · weekly check for week of Mon '+fd(r.week);
 $('#sum').innerHTML=r.sites.map(function(s){var l=r.last[s],c=r.checks[s],cls=!l?'none':c?'':'late';
  return'<div class="sc '+cls+'"><b>'+E(s)+'</b>'+(l?'Last update '+fd(l[2])+(l[1]?' · '+E(l[1]):''):'No updates yet')+'<br>'+(c?'✓ Weekly check '+sd(c[0])+' · '+E(c[1]):'✗ Weekly check not done')+'</div>'}).join('');
 var keepSite=$('#asite').value;
 $('#asite').innerHTML='<option value="">All sites</option>'+r.sites.map(function(s){return'<option>'+E(s)+'</option>'}).join('');
 if(r.sites.indexOf(keepSite)>=0)$('#asite').value=keepSite;
 var tt=(r.transit||[]).length,sh=(r.shortages||[]).length;
 $('#oAlert').hidden=!(tt||sh);$('#oAlert').textContent='⚠ '+(tt?tt+' transfer'+(tt==1?'':'s')+' in transit':'')+(tt&&sh?' · ':'')+(sh?sh+' shortage'+(sh==1?'':'s')+' waiting for the sender':'')+' – tap to see';
 var mcount=0,mw2=0,mm2=0,msites={};Object.keys(r.mach||{}).forEach(function(id){Object.keys(r.mach[id]).forEach(function(s){var v=r.mach[id][s];mcount++;mw2+=v[1];mm2+=v[0];msites[s]=1})});
 $('#oMach').hidden=!mcount;$('#oMach').innerHTML='🔧 <b>'+mcount+' machine'+(mcount==1?'':'s')+' need'+(mcount==1?'s':'')+' attention</b> – '+[mw2?mw2+' at workshop':'',mm2?mm2+' need'+(mm2==1?'s':'')+' maintenance':''].filter(String).join(', ')+' · '+Object.keys(msites).join(', ')+'. Tap to see.';
 drawAll();showO(oTab);msg('');
}
function drawAll(){
 if(!all)return;
 var one=$('#asite').value,sites=one?[one]:all.sites;
 $('#alist').innerHTML=items.map(function(it){
  var id=String(it[0]),src=imgs[it[5]],qq=all.q[id]||{},iq=(all.idle||{})[id]||{},mq=(all.mach||{})[id]||{},tot=0,idl=0,any=false,ch='',mw=0,mm=0;
  sites.forEach(function(s){var v=qq[s];if(!v||empty(v[0]))return;tot+=Number(v[0]);any=true;
   var old=days(v[1],all.date)>7&&!all.checks[s],iv=iq[s];if(iv)idl+=iv[0];
   ch+='<span'+(old?' class="old"':'')+' title="updated '+E(fd(v[1],true))+'">'+E(s)+' <b>'+E(v[0])+'</b></span>'+(iv?'<span class="idle" title="idle since '+E(fd(iv[1],true))+'">'+E(s)+' idle <b>'+E(iv[0])+'</b></span>':'');
   var mv=mq[s];if(mv){mw+=mv[1];mm+=mv[0];
    if(mv[1]>0)ch+='<span class="mrp" title="'+E((mv[2]||'')+(mv[3]?' · '+mv[3]:''))+'">🔧 '+E(s)+' at workshop <b>'+E(mv[1])+'</b> · '+days(mv[5],all.date)+'d</span>';
    if(mv[0]>0)ch+='<span class="mmt" title="'+E(mv[2]||'')+'">⚠️ '+E(s)+' maintenance <b>'+E(mv[0])+'</b> · '+days(mv[4],all.date)+'d</span>'}});
  idl=Math.round(idl*1000)/1000;
  var tn=0;(all.transit||[]).forEach(function(t){if(one&&t.from!==one&&t.to!==one)return;t.lines.forEach(function(l){if(String(l.itemId)===id&&l.status==='In transit')tn+=l.qty})});
  if(tn)ch+='<span class="tr">In transit <b>'+Math.round(tn*1000)/1000+'</b></span>';
  tot=Math.round(tot*1000)/1000;
  var rq=(all.rent||{})[id]||{},rs=Object.keys(rq).filter(function(s){return!one||s===one}).sort(),rn=0;rs.forEach(function(s){rn+=rq[s]});
  var rtx=rn?'<div class="rentb">🚜 +'+rn+' rented'+(!one?' · '+rs.map(function(s){return E(s)+' '+rq[s]}).join(' · '):'')+' · not in totals</div>':'';
  var svk=svcKind(it[1]);if(svk){var K=SVK[svk],sv=(all.svc||{})[id]||{},svs=Object.keys(sv).filter(function(s){return!one||s===one}).sort(),sq=0,sa=0;svs.forEach(function(s){sq+=sv[s][0];sa+=sv[s][1]});
   rtx+='<span class="svl" style="color:'+K.c+';background:'+K.bg+';border-color:'+K.c+'">'+K.ic+' '+K.verb+' in '+MN3[+String(all.date||today).slice(5,7)-1]+': '+(svs.length?svs.map(function(s){return E(s)+' '+svN(sv[s][0])+(svk==='fuel'?' L':'')}).join(' · ')+(svs.length>1?' = '+svN(sq)+(svk==='fuel'?' L':''):'')+(sa?' · AED '+svN(sa):''):'none')+'</span>'}
  var on=sel.has(id);
  return '<div class="it'+(on?' picked':'')+(mw>0?' m-rp':mm>0?' m-mt':'')+'" data-id="'+E(id)+'" data-q="'+E((it[1]+' '+it[2]).toLowerCase())+'" data-c="'+E(it[2])+'" data-s="'+(tot>0?1:0)+'" data-idl="'+idl+'" data-i="'+(idl>0?1:0)+'" data-m="'+(mw+mm>0?1:0)+'"><input type="checkbox" class="selbox" data-sel="'+E(id)+'"'+(on?' checked':'')+' aria-label="Select '+E(it[1])+'">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+
   '<div class="nm">'+E(it[1])+'<small>'+E(it[2])+' · '+E(it[4])+'</small>'+(ch?'<div class="chips">'+ch+'</div>':'')+rtx+(Object.keys(mq).filter(function(s){return!one||s===one}).map(function(s){var mv=mq[s];return mv[2]?'<small class="mst'+(mv[1]>0?'':' mt')+'">'+E(s)+': '+E(mv[2])+(mv[1]>0&&mv[3]?' · '+E(mv[3]):'')+'</small>':''}).join(''))+
   '<label class="reqb">Required qty<input class="reqin" data-req="'+E(id)+'" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" placeholder="–" value="'+E(reqs.get(id)||'')+'" aria-label="Required quantity for '+E(it[1])+'"><span class="rbal"></span></label></div>'+
   '<div class="tot">'+(any?tot:'–')+'<small>'+(one?'Qty':'Total')+(idl?'</small><small class="idl">'+idl+' idle':'')+'</small></div></div>'}).join('');
 document.querySelectorAll('#alist .it').forEach(rbalUpd);
 afilt();
}
/* Required qty: live balance against the IDLE qty (same as the PDF) */
function rbalUpd(row){var sp=row.querySelector('.rbal');if(!sp)return;var v=reqs.get(row.dataset.id);if(v==null||v===''){sp.textContent='';sp.className='rbal';return}
 var idl=Number(row.dataset.idl)||0,b=Math.round((idl-Number(v))*1000)/1000;sp.textContent='Idle '+idl+' → '+(b<0?'−'+(-b)+' short':b>0?'+'+b+' extra':'OK');sp.className='rbal '+(b<0?'sh':'ok')}
function afilt(){
 var q=$('#aq').value.toLowerCase(),c=$('#acat').value,s=$('#astock').checked,ai=$('#aidle').checked,am=$('#amach').checked,n=0;
 document.querySelectorAll('#alist .it').forEach(function(e){var ok=(!q||e.dataset.q.indexOf(q)>=0)&&(!c||e.dataset.c===c)&&(!s||e.dataset.s==='1')&&(!ai||e.dataset.i==='1')&&(!am||e.dataset.m==='1');e.hidden=!ok;if(ok)n++});
 $('#acnt').textContent=n+' items';
 selUI();
}
/* ---------- ALL SITES: select items + PDF ---------- */
var sel=new Set(),oTab='stock',pdfRes=null,reqs=new Map();/* required qty: only on this screen, never saved */
function shownIds(){return Array.prototype.filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('#alist .it'),function(e){return!e.hidden}).map(function(e){return e.dataset.id})}
function selUI(){
 var ids=shownIds();$('#aselShown').checked=ids.length>0&&ids.every(function(i){return sel.has(i)});
 $('#pdfbar').hidden=!(isAll()&&opened&&oTab==='stock'&&sel.size);
 var nr=0;reqs.forEach(function(v,k){if(sel.has(k))nr++});
 $('#pdfCnt').textContent=sel.size+' item'+(sel.size==1?'':'s')+' selected'+($('#asite').value?' · '+$('#asite').value:' · all sites')+(nr?' · '+nr+' with required qty':'');
}
function setSel(id,on){if(on)sel.add(id);else sel.delete(id);var row=document.querySelector('#alist .it[data-id="'+id+'"]');if(row){row.classList.toggle('picked',on);row.querySelector('.selbox').checked=on}}
$('#alist').addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target.closest('.reqb'))return;var row=e.target.closest('.it');if(!row)return;var id=row.dataset.id;setSel(id,e.target.classList.contains('selbox')?e.target.checked:!sel.has(id));selUI()});
$('#alist').addEventListener('input',function(e){var i=e.target;if(!i.classList.contains('reqin'))return;
 var v=i.value.replace(/,/g,'.').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'');if(v!==i.value)i.value=v;if(v.trim()==='')reqs.delete(i.dataset.req);else reqs.set(i.dataset.req,v.trim());rbalUpd(i.closest('.it'));selUI()});
$('#aselShown').onchange=function(){var on=this.checked;shownIds().forEach(function(i){setSel(i,on)});selUI()};
$('#aclear').onclick=function(){Array.from(sel).forEach(function(i){setSel(i,false)});selUI()};
$('#pdfBtn').onclick=async function(){

 b.disabled=false;b.textContent='Create PDF';
 if(!r.ok){$('#pdfCnt').textContent=r.error;return}
 pdfRes=r;$('#pdfMsg').textContent=r.name+' · '+r.count+' item'+(r.count==1?'':'s')+(r.url?' · also saved in your Google Drive folder “WTC Stock PDFs”':'');

};
$('#pdfDl').onclick=function(){
 if(!pdfRes)return;
 try{var bin=atob(pdfRes.b64),u=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(var i=0;i<bin.length;i++)u[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
  var url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([u],{type:'application/pdf'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=pdfRes.name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();
  setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(url);a.remove()},5000)}catch(e){$('#pdfMsg').textContent='Download was blocked on this device – use Open in Google Drive.'}
};

var skSel=new Set();

function skMode(on){document.body.classList.toggle('pdfmode',on);$('#skbar').hidden=!on;$('#myPdf').hidden=on;if(!on)skSel.clear();skUI();if(on)msg('Tick the items you want, then tap Create PDF.')}

 if(nEd()){msg('Save your changes first, then make the PDF.');return}
 skMode(true);
};

$('#skAll').onclick=function(){document.querySelectorAll('#list .it').forEach(function(e){if(!e.hidden&&Number(e.querySelector('input[data-r]').value)>0)skSel.add(e.dataset.id)});skUI()};

 if(!ids.length){msg('Tick at least one item (or tap Select all shown).');return}
 if(nEd()){msg('Save your changes first, then make the PDF.');return}
 var b=this;b.disabled=true;b.textContent='Creating PDF…';msg('Creating the PDF…');

 pdfRes=r;$('#pdfMsg').textContent=r.name+' · '+r.count+' item'+(r.count==1?'':'s')+' · tap Download to save it on your phone';
 $('#pdfDrive').hidden=true;$('#pdfPop').hidden=false;skMode(false);

var XT={received:{label:'RECEIVED FROM SUPPLIER',help:'Deliveries from suppliers to each site.',sup:'Supplier',f:'info1'},
 used:{label:'USED / CONSUMED',help:'Consumables used on each site.',sup:'Used for (job / area)',f:'info1'},
 damaged:{label:'DAMAGED',help:'Items written off as damaged.',sup:'Reason',f:'info1'},
 transfers:{label:'TRANSFERS BETWEEN SITES',help:'Items sent from one site to another.',sup:'Status',f:'status'}};
var xtype='received',txData=[],txFor='';
function showO(t){oTab=t;try{sessionStorage.setItem('wtc_otab',t)}catch(e){}document.querySelectorAll('#otabs button').forEach(function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.o===t)});
 $('#oStock').hidden=t!=='stock';$('#oTx').hidden=t!=='tx';$('#oRent').hidden=t!=='rent';if(t==='rent'&&!ORN)loadORent();$('#oEx').hidden=t!=='ex';if(t==='ex'&&(!X||xRole!=='office'))loadOEx();$('#oNt').hidden=t!=='nt';if(t==='nt'&&!ONT)loadONt();$('#oAs').hidden=t!=='as';if(t==='as'&&!OAS)loadOAs();$('#oSi').hidden=t!=='si';if(t==='si'&&!OSI)loadOSi();$('#oPpe').hidden=t!=='ppe';if(t==='ppe'&&!PPO)loadOPpe();selUI();if(t==='tx'&&txFor!==xtype+'|'+$('#txFrom').value+'|'+$('#txTo').value)loadTx()}
$('#otabs').addEventListener('click',function(e){var b=e.target.closest('[data-o]');if(b)showO(b.dataset.o)});
$('#oAlert').onclick=function(){setX('transfers');showO('tx')};
function setX(t){xtype=t;document.querySelectorAll('#txType button').forEach(function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.x===t)});
 var x=XT[t];$('#txBan').className='banner '+(t==='transfers'?'k-tr':'k-'+t);$('#txBan').innerHTML='<b>'+E(x.label)+'</b>'+E(x.help);$('#txSupL').textContent=x.sup;$('#txSup').value='';txFor=''}
$('#txType').addEventListener('click',function(e){var b=e.target.closest('[data-x]');if(b){setX(b.dataset.x);loadTx()}});
async function loadTx(){
 if(!$('#txFrom').value){$('#txFrom').value=addDays(today,-30);$('#txTo').value=today;$('#txFrom').max=today;$('#txTo').max=today}
 var t=xtype,k=t+'|'+$('#txFrom').value+'|'+$('#txTo').value;$('#txMsg').textContent='Loading…';
 var r;try{r=await run('loadOfficeLog',$('#pin').value.trim(),t,$('#txFrom').value,$('#txTo').value)}catch(e){$('#txMsg').textContent='Connection problem. Please try again.';return}
 if(t!==xtype)return;
 if(!r.ok){$('#txMsg').textContent=r.error;return}
 txData=r.list;txFor=k;$('#txMsg').textContent='';
 var ks=$('#txSite').value;$('#txSite').innerHTML='<option value="">All sites</option>'+r.sites.map(function(s){return'<option>'+E(s)+'</option>'}).join('');$('#txSite').value=ks;
 var vals={};txData.forEach(function(g){var v=t==='transfers'?g.status:g.info1;if(v)vals[v]=1});
 if(t==='transfers')vals['Shortage waiting']=1;
 var kv=$('#txSup').value;$('#txSup').innerHTML='<option value="">All</option>'+Object.keys(vals).sort().map(function(v){return'<option>'+E(v)+'</option>'}).join('');$('#txSup').value=kv;
 drawTx();
}
function drawTx(){
 var t=xtype,site=$('#txSite').value,sv=$('#txSup').value,iq=$('#txItem').value.trim().toLowerCase(),q=$('#txQ').value.trim().toLowerCase(),sc=$('#txCanc').checked;
 var isC=function(g){return String(g.status).indexOf('Cancelled')===0};
 var l=txData.filter(function(g){
  if(isC(g)&&!sc)return false;
  if(t==='transfers'){if(site&&g.from!==site&&g.to!==site)return false;if(sv&&(sv==='Shortage waiting'?!(g.pending>0):g.status!==sv))return false}
  else{if(site&&g.site!==site)return false;if(sv&&g.info1!==sv)return false}
  if(iq&&!g.lines.some(function(x){return has(x.item,iq)}))return false;
  return!q||has(g.ref||g.id,q)||has(g.info1,q)||has(g.info2,q)||has(g.note,q)||has(g.by,q)||has(g.recBy,q)||has(g.status,q)});
 var live=l.filter(function(g){return!isC(g)}),byItem={},bySite={},lines=0;
 live.forEach(function(g){
  var sk=t==='transfers'?g.from+' → '+g.to:g.site;bySite[sk]=bySite[sk]||{n:0};bySite[sk].n++;
  g.lines.forEach(function(x){if(iq&&!has(x.item,iq))return;if(String(x.status||'').indexOf('Cancelled')===0)return;lines++;var k=x.item+'|'+x.unit;
   byItem[k]=byItem[k]||{item:x.item,unit:x.unit,q:0,n:0};byItem[k].q=Math.round((byItem[k].q+Number(x.qty))*1000)/1000;byItem[k].n++})});
 var it=Object.keys(byItem).map(function(k){return byItem[k]}).sort(function(a,b){return b.q-a.q}),st=Object.keys(bySite).sort(function(a,b){return bySite[b].n-bySite[a].n});
 var word={received:'received',used:'used',damaged:'damaged',transfers:'sent'}[t];
 $('#txSum').innerHTML='<div class="stat"><div><b>'+live.length+'</b>entries</div><div><b>'+lines+'</b>item lines</div><div><b>'+it.length+'</b>different items</div></div>'+
  (it.length?'<h3>Total '+word+' by item'+(it.length>15?' (top 15)':'')+'</h3><table class="stbl"><tr><th>Item</th><th class="n">Qty</th><th>Unit</th><th class="n">Entries</th></tr>'+
   it.slice(0,15).map(function(r){return'<tr><td>'+E(r.item)+'</td><td class="n"><b>'+r.q+'</b></td><td>'+E(r.unit)+'</td><td class="n">'+r.n+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</table>':'')+
  (st.length?'<h3>'+(t==='transfers'?'By route':'By site')+'</h3><table class="stbl"><tr><th>'+(t==='transfers'?'From → To':'Site')+'</th><th class="n">Entries</th></tr>'+
   st.map(function(k){return'<tr><td>'+E(k)+'</td><td class="n">'+bySite[k].n+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</table>':'');
 $('#txListH').textContent=l.length+' entr'+(l.length==1?'y':'ies')+' · '+fd($('#txFrom').value,true)+' – '+fd($('#txTo').value,true)+(l.length>200?' (first 200 shown)':'');
 $('#txList').innerHTML=l.length?l.slice(0,200).map(function(g){return t==='transfers'?tcard(g,'all'):mcard(g,true)}).join(''):'<p class="msg">Nothing matches these filters.</p>';
}
$('#txFrom').onchange=loadTx;$('#txTo').onchange=loadTx;
['#txSite','#txSup','#txCanc'].forEach(function(k){$(k).onchange=drawTx});$('#txItem').oninput=drawTx;$('#txQ').oninput=drawTx;
$('#txReset').onclick=function(){$('#txSite').value='';$('#txSup').value='';$('#txItem').value='';$('#txQ').value='';$('#txCanc').checked=false;
 $('#txFrom').value=addDays(today,-30);$('#txTo').value=today;loadTx()};

var okTimer=null;
var okT0=0;
function hideOk(){$('#okPop').hidden=true;$('#okPop').classList.remove('saving');clearTimeout(okTimer)}
/* opens the box at once while Google saves; it turns green when the save is confirmed */
function showSaving(){
 okT0=Date.now();clearTimeout(okTimer);var p=$('#okPop');p.classList.add('saving');
 $('#okI').innerHTML='<span class="spin"></span>';$('#okH').textContent='SAVING…';$('#okT').textContent='Please wait – do not close the page';$('#okMsg').textContent='';p.hidden=false;
}
function bigOk(title,m){
 acts++;ping();
 var p=$('#okPop');p.classList.remove('saving');$('#okI').textContent='✓';$('#okH').textContent='SUCCESSFUL';
 var secs=okT0?' (saved in '+((Date.now()-okT0)/1000).toFixed(1)+' s)':'';okT0=0;
 $('#okT').textContent=title;$('#okMsg').textContent=m.replace(/^✓\s*/,'')+secs;p.hidden=false;
 try{if(navigator.vibrate)navigator.vibrate(150)}catch(e){}
 clearTimeout(okTimer);okTimer=setTimeout(hideOk,8000);$('#okBtn').focus();
}

/* ---------- sign-in session: who, how long, sign out ---------- */
var sess=null,sessTimer=null,acts=0,soSure=false;
function dev(){var u=navigator.userAgent||'';return(/iPhone|iPad/.test(u)?'iPhone/iPad':/Android/.test(u)?'Android':/Windows/.test(u)?'Windows PC':/Mac/.test(u)?'Mac':'Other')+' · '+(/Edg\//.test(u)?'Edge':/Chrome|CriOS/.test(u)?'Chrome':/Firefox/.test(u)?'Firefox':/Safari/.test(u)?'Safari':'Browser')}
async function afterLogin(){
 $('#form').hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('login');$('#who').hidden=false;soSure=false;$('#signout').textContent='Sign out';
 $('#whoT').textContent=isAll()?'🏢 Office · ALL SITES':isPV()?'👁 '+$('#name').value+' · PPE (view only)':(isX()?'♻ ':'👤 ')+$('#name').value+' · '+$('#site').value;
 if(sess)return;
 sess='pending';acts=0;
 var r;try{r=await run('startSession',$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),isAll()?'Office':$('#name').value,dev())}catch(e){r=null}
 sess=r&&r.ok?r.sid:null;
 clearInterval(sessTimer);if(sess)sessTimer=setInterval(ping,120000);lastAct=Date.now();keepSave();
}
function ping(){if(sess&&sess!=='pending')run('pingSession',sess,acts).catch(function(){})}
function unsaved(){return nEd()||basket.length||mbasket.length}
$('#signout').onclick=function(){
 if(unsaved()&&!soSure){soSure=true;this.textContent='Unsaved changes – tap again';return}
 signOutNow('Signed out')};
async function signOutNow(how){
 var id=sess,b=$('#signout');b.disabled=true;keepClear();
 if(id&&id!=='pending'){try{await run('endSession',id,how,acts)}catch(e){}}
 clearInterval(sessTimer);sess=null;acts=0;b.disabled=false;
 edits.clear();iedits.clear();clearBasket();clearMBasket();sel.clear();reqs.clear();txData=[];txFor='';showO('stock');
 $('#pin').value='';$('#site').value='';$('#adate').value=today;setNames();modeUI();closeAll();
 $('#who').hidden=true;$('#form').hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('login');qShow(true);$('#pdfPop').hidden=true;window.scrollTo(0,0);msg(how==='Timed out'?'Signed out after 15 minutes without activity. Type your site, PIN and name to sign in again.':'Signed out. Type your site, PIN and name to sign in again.');
};

/* ---------- rented machines (hired from rental companies – never part of our stock) ---------- */
var RN={list:[],types:[],cos:[]},ORN=null,rnType='',rnItem='',rnEditRef='',rnPhoto='',orSt='on';
function rnN(x){return Math.round(Number(x)||0).toLocaleString('en-US')}
function rnIcon(o){if(o.itemId&&imap[o.itemId]&&imgs[imap[o.itemId][5]])return'<img alt="" src="'+imgs[imap[o.itemId][5]]+'">';
 var t=(RN.types||[]).filter(function(x){return x[0]===o.machine})[0];return E(t&&t[1]||'🛠️')}
function rnRate(o){return o.rate?'AED '+rnN(o.rate)+' / '+o.per.toLowerCase()+(o.qty>1?' each':''):'–'}
function rnCard(o,office){
 var late=o.late>0&&!o.ret;
 return '<div class="rc'+(late?' od':'')+'" data-ref="'+E(o.ref)+'"><div class="t"><div class="em">'+rnIcon(o)+'</div><div class="nm">'+E(o.machine)+(o.size?' '+E(o.size):'')+(o.qty>1?' <span class="q">× '+o.qty+'</span>':'')+
  '<small>'+(o.itemId?'<span class="rpill own">our item '+E(o.itemId)+'</span>':'')+E(o.ref)+' · '+E(o.company)+'</small></div></div>'+
  '<div class="g"><div>Plate / fleet no.<b>'+E(o.plate||'–')+'</b></div><div>Operator<b>'+E(o.op||'–')+'</b></div>'+
  '<div>On hire since<b>'+fd(o.on)+' · '+o.days+' day'+(o.days==1?'':'s')+'</b></div>'+
  (o.ret?'<div>Returned<b>'+fd(o.ret)+'</b></div>':'<div>Expected return<b'+(late?' style="color:#B8274A"':'')+'>'+(o.exp?fd(o.exp):'–')+(late?' <span class="rpill od">'+o.late+' day'+(o.late==1?'':'s')+' late</span>':'')+'</b></div>')+
  (office?'<div>Site<b>'+E(o.site)+'</b></div>':'')+'<div>Rate<b>'+rnRate(o)+'</b></div>'+(o.cost?'<div>Rent so far (est.)<b>AED '+rnN(o.cost)+'</b></div>':'')+(o.lpo?'<div>LPO / DN<b>'+E(o.lpo)+'</b></div>':'')+'<div>Added by<b>'+E(o.by)+'</b></div></div>'+
  (o.photo?'<a href="'+E(o.photo)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">📷 Photo</a>':'')+
  (!office&&!o.ret?'<div class="bt"><button data-ra="edit">✎ Edit</button><button data-ra="move">⇄ Move site</button><button data-ra="ret" class="x">↩ Returned</button></div>':'')+'</div>'}
async function loadRent(quiet){
 if(isAll()||!opened)return;
 if(!quiet&&!RN.list.length)$('#rnLoad').textContent='Loading…';
 var r;try{r=await run('loadRentals',$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),$('#name').value)}catch(e){$('#rnLoad').textContent='Connection problem. Please try again.';return}
 if(!r.ok){$('#rnLoad').textContent=r.error;return}
 $('#rnLoad').textContent='';rnApply(r);
}
function rnApply(r){RN=r;var on=r.list.filter(function(o){return!o.ret}),late=on.filter(function(o){return o.late>0}).length,ret=r.list.length-on.length;
 var b=$('#rnBadge');b.hidden=!on.length;b.textContent=on.length+' on site'+(late?' · '+late+' late':'');
 $('#rnSum').innerHTML='<div><b>'+on.length+'</b>on site now</div><div'+(late?' class="bad"':'')+'><b>'+late+'</b>late return</div><div><b>'+ret+'</b>returned (30 days)</div>';
 on.sort(function(a,b){return(b.late-a.late)||(a.on<b.on?-1:1)});
 $('#rnList').innerHTML=on.length?on.map(function(o){return rnCard(o,false)}).join(''):'<p class="msg">No rented machines at your site. Tap ＋ Add rented machine when one arrives.</p>';
 var rl=r.list.filter(function(o){return o.ret}).sort(function(a,b){return a.ret<b.ret?1:-1});
 $('#rnRet').hidden=!rl.length;$('#rnRet').innerHTML='<b style="color:var(--fg)">Returned (last 30 days)</b>'+rl.map(function(o){return'<div><span>'+(o.qty>1?o.qty+' × ':'')+E(o.machine)+' · '+E(o.company)+'</span><span>'+fd(o.on)+' – '+fd(o.ret)+' · '+o.days+'d</span></div>'}).join('');
 if(!rnEditRef)rnTypesDraw();rnCoDraw();
}
var RNL=[],rnK=0;/* machines in this delivery */
function rnCount(key){return RNL.filter(function(l){return(l.itemId?'i'+l.itemId:'t'+l.machine)===key}).length}
function rnTypesDraw(){var q=$('#rnIq').value.trim().toLowerCase(),cat=$('#rnCat').value;
 var ty=cat&&cat!=='*'?[]:(RN.types||[]).filter(function(t){return!q||t[0].toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0});
 var its=cat==='*'?[]:items.filter(function(it){return it[9]!==false&&(!cat||it[2]===cat)&&(!q||(it[1]+' '+it[2]+' '+it[3]+' '+it[0]).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0)});
 var cnt=function(k){var n=rnCount(k);return n?'<span class="cnt">'+n+'</span>':''};
 $('#rnTypes').innerHTML=ty.map(function(t){var n=rnCount('t'+t[0]);return'<button data-t="'+E(t[0])+'"'+(n?' class="on"':'')+'>'+cnt('t'+t[0])+'<i>'+E(t[1]||'🛠️')+'</i>'+E(t[0])+'</button>'}).join('')+
  '<button data-t="" class="oth'+(rnType==='*'?' on':'')+'"><i>✚</i>Other – not in this list</button>'+
  (its.length?'<div class="more" style="text-align:left;font-weight:800;color:var(--fg)">🧰 Our item list · '+its.length+' item'+(its.length==1?'':'s')+'</div>':'')+
  its.map(function(it){var id=String(it[0]),src=imgs[it[5]],n=rnCount('i'+id);return'<button data-i="'+E(id)+'"'+(n?' class="on"':'')+'>'+cnt('i'+id)+(src?'<img alt="" loading="lazy" src="'+src+'">':'<i>🧰</i>')+E(it[1])+'<small>our item · we have '+(base(id)||0)+'</small></button>'}).join('')+
  (q&&!ty.length&&!its.length?'<div class="more">Nothing found – tap Other and type the name.</div>':'')}
function rnCatDraw(){var c=$('#rnCat'),v=c.value,cats=[];items.forEach(function(it){if(it[9]!==false&&cats.indexOf(it[2])<0)cats.push(it[2])});
 c.innerHTML='<option value="">All machines and items</option><option value="*">🚜 Rental machines only</option>'+cats.map(function(x){return'<option>'+E(x)+'</option>'}).join('');if(v)c.value=v}
function rnCoDraw(){var c=$('#rnCo'),v=c.value;c.innerHTML='<option value="">Choose the rental company…</option>'+(RN.cos||[]).map(function(x){return'<option>'+E(x)+'</option>'}).join('')+'<option value="*">＋ New company (type it)</option>';if(v&&(v==='*'||RN.cos.indexOf(v)>=0))c.value=v}

 var m=function(t){$('#rnMsg').textContent=t};
 if(rnType==='*'&&$('#rnOth').value.trim())return m('Tap ＋ Add next to the machine name first.');

  if(!(Number(l.qty)>=1)||Math.floor(Number(l.qty))!=Number(l.qty))return m(nm+'How many: type 1 or more.');
  if(!l.op)return m(nm+'Choose With operator or Without operator.')}
 var common={company:$('#rnCo').value==='*'?$('#rnCoNew').value:$('#rnCo').value,on:$('#rnOn').value,exp:$('#rnExp').value,lpo:$('#rnLpo').value,photo:rnPhoto};

 m('');showSaving();
 var lines=RNL.map(function(l){return{machine:l.itemId?'':l.machine,itemId:l.itemId||'',qty:l.qty,size:l.size||'',plate:l.plate||'',op:l.op,per:l.per,rate:l.rate||''}});

};

function orDraw(){if(!ORN)return;var s=$('#orSite').value,c=$('#orCo').value,q=$('#orQ').value.trim().toLowerCase();
 document.querySelectorAll('#orSt button').forEach(function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.s===orSt)});
 var l=ORN.list.filter(function(o){return(!s||o.site===s)&&(!c||o.company===c)&&(orSt===''||(orSt==='on'&&!o.ret)||(orSt==='late'&&!o.ret&&o.late>0)||(orSt==='ret'&&o.ret))&&
  (!q||[o.machine,o.size,o.plate,o.lpo,o.ref,o.by,o.company,o.site].join(' ').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0)});
 l.sort(function(a,b){return a.site<b.site?-1:a.site>b.site?1:(b.late-a.late)||(a.on<b.on?-1:1)});
 var h='',last=null,cnt={};l.forEach(function(o){cnt[o.site]=(cnt[o.site]||0)+1});
 l.forEach(function(o){if(o.site!==last){last=o.site;h+='<div class="orgrp">'+E(o.site)+' <span>'+cnt[o.site]+' rental'+(cnt[o.site]==1?'':'s')+'</span></div>'}h+=rnCard(o,true)});
 $('#orList').innerHTML=h||'<p class="msg">Nothing to show.</p>';$('#orCnt').textContent=l.length+' rental'+(l.length==1?'':'s')+(orSt==='ret'||orSt===''?' · returned ones: last 120 days':'');
}

/* ---------- stay signed in when the page is refreshed (this tab only); sign out after 15 minutes without activity ---------- */
var IDLE_MS=15*60000,lastAct=Date.now();
function sget(){try{return JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('wtc_s')||'null')}catch(e){return null}}
function sset(o){try{sessionStorage.setItem('wtc_s',JSON.stringify(o))}catch(e){}}
function keepClear(){try{sessionStorage.removeItem('wtc_s')}catch(e){}}
function keepSave(){if(!sess||sess==='pending')return;sset({site:$('#site').value,pin:$('#pin').value.trim(),name:isAll()?'':$('#name').value,sid:sess,t:lastAct,acts:acts})}
function touch(){if(!sess||sess==='pending')return;var now=Date.now();if(now-lastAct>IDLE_MS){idleCheck();return}lastAct=now;var s=sget();if(s&&now-(s.t||0)>15000){s.t=now;s.acts=acts;sset(s)}}
function idleCheck(){if(sess&&sess!=='pending'&&Date.now()-lastAct>IDLE_MS)signOutNow('Timed out')}
['pointerdown','keydown','input','touchstart','wheel'].forEach(function(ev){document.addEventListener(ev,touch,{passive:true,capture:true})});
setInterval(idleCheck,20000);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',function(){if(document.visibilityState==='visible')idleCheck()});
async function restoreLogin(){
 var done=function(ok){document.body.classList.remove('restoring');if(!ok){document.body.classList.add('login');$('#form').hidden=false;$('#who').hidden=true}$('#boot').hidden=true};
 var sv=sget();if(!sv){done(false);return}
 if(!(Date.now()-(sv.t||0)<IDLE_MS)||allSites.indexOf(sv.site)<0&&xStores.indexOf(sv.site)<0&&!(sv.site===PV&&PPE_VIEW)){keepClear();if(sv.sid)run('endSession',sv.sid,'Timed out',sv.acts||0).catch(function(){});done(false);msg('Signed out after 15 minutes without activity. Type your site, PIN and name to sign in again.');return}
 $('#site').value=sv.site;setNames();modeUI();$('#pin').value=sv.pin;if(sv.name)$('#name').value=sv.name;
 sess=sv.sid;acts=sv.acts||0;lastAct=Date.now();var tb=null;try{tb=sessionStorage.getItem(isAll()?'wtc_otab':'wtc_tab')}catch(e){}
 if(isAll())await loadAll();else if(isX())await loadX(false);else if(isPV())await loadPV(true);else await loadMine(false);
 if(!opened){keepClear();sess=null;$('#pin').value='';done(false);return}
 done(true);try{if(tb&&!isX()&&!isPV()){if(isAll())showO(tb);else showTab(tb)}}catch(e){}
 clearInterval(sessTimer);sessTimer=setInterval(ping,120000);ping();keepSave();
}

function xImg(f,max,q){return new Promise(function(res){var rd=new FileReader();rd.onload=function(){var im=new Image();im.onload=function(){var s=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(im.width,im.height)),c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.round(im.width*s);c.height=Math.round(im.height*s);c.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);res(c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q))};im.onerror=function(){res('')};im.src=rd.result};rd.onerror=function(){res('')};rd.readAsDataURL(f)})}
function xThumb(src){return new Promise(function(res){if(!src){res('');return}var im=new Image();im.onload=function(){var z=160,c=document.createElement('canvas'),s=Math.min(im.width,im.height);c.width=z;c.height=z;c.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,(im.width-s)/2,(im.height-s)/2,s,s,0,0,z,z);res(c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.55))};im.onerror=function(){res('')};im.src=src})}

var PP_ORD=['COVER ALL','SAFETY JACKET','SAFETY SHOES','HELMET'];
function ppIco(t,c){return PP_IMG[t]?'<img class="ppico'+(c?' '+c:'')+'" alt="" src="'+PP_IMG[t]+'">':'<span class="ppico ppemo'+(c?' '+c:'')+'">🦺</span>'}
function ppTN(t){return{'COVER ALL':'Cover all','SAFETY JACKET':'Safety jacket','SAFETY SHOES':'Safety shoes','HELMET':'Helmet','MASK':'Mask'}[t]||(t?t.charAt(0)+t.slice(1).toLowerCase():'Other')}
function ppOn(){return !!PPE_SITE&&!isAll()&&$('#site').value===PPE_SITE}
function ppWho(o){return o?['ALL SITES',$('#pin').value.trim(),'']:[$('#site').value,$('#pin').value.trim(),$('#name').value]}
function ppP(o){return o?PPO:PPS}
function ppAddM(ds,m){var p=String(ds).split('-'),d=new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0],+p[1]-1+Number(m),1)),l=new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(),d.getUTCMonth()+1,0)).getUTCDate();d.setUTCDate(Math.min(+p[2],l));return d.toISOString().slice(0,10)}
function ppMon(P,t){var m=P.rules.months[t];return m!=null?m:P.rules.other}
function ppMT(m){return m%12===0?(m/12)+(m===12?' year':' years'):m+' months'}
function ppAgo(ds,t){var d=Math.round((Date.parse(t)-Date.parse(ds))/864e5);return d<1?'today':d<45?d+' day'+(d==1?'':'s')+' ago':(d/30.4).toFixed(1)+' months ago'}
function ppN(n){return String(Math.round(Number(n)*1000)/1000)}
function ppTypes(P){var s={};P.items.forEach(function(i){if(i.active&&i.type)s[i.type]=1});return PP_ORD.filter(function(t){return s[t]}).concat(Object.keys(s).filter(function(t){return PP_ORD.indexOf(t)<0}).sort())}
var PP_SZ=['XS','S','M','L','XL','2XL','3XL','4XL','5XL'];
function ppSzK(s){var i=PP_SZ.indexOf(String(s).toUpperCase());if(i>=0)return i;var n=parseFloat(s);return isNaN(n)?99:20+n}
function ppTO(t){var i=PP_ORD.indexOf(t);return i<0?9:i}
function ppSort(a,b){return ppTO(a.type)-ppTO(b.type)||a.type.localeCompare(b.type)||a.cat.localeCompare(b.cat)||ppSzK(a.size)-ppSzK(b.size)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name)}

function ppStockList(o){var P=ppP(o),el=$(o?'#ppOList':'#ppList');if(!el)return;var q=ppQ.trim().toLowerCase(),last='',h='';
 var l=P.items.filter(function(i){if(!i.active&&!o)return false;if(ppTF==='HIDDEN'){if(i.active)return false}else if(ppTF==='LOW'){if(!(i.bal<=0||(i.min!==''&&i.bal<i.min)))return false}else if(ppTF&&i.type!==ppTF)return false;
  return !q||(i.name+' '+i.code+' '+i.size+' '+i.cat+' '+i.type).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0});
 var has={};l.forEach(function(i){if(i.bal>0)has[i.type+'|'+i.cat]=1});l.sort(function(a,b){return ppTO(a.type)-ppTO(b.type)||a.type.localeCompare(b.type)||(has[b.type+'|'+b.cat]?1:0)-(has[a.type+'|'+a.cat]?1:0)||ppSort(a,b)});
 l.forEach(function(i){var g=i.type+'|'+i.cat;if(g!==last){if(last)h+='</div>';last=g;h+='<div class="ppgrp"><div class="ppgh">'+ppIco(i.type)+E(ppTN(i.type).toUpperCase()+(i.cat?' · '+i.cat:''))+'</div>'}
  var low=i.min!==''&&i.bal<i.min,z=i.bal<=0;
  h+='<div class="pprow'+(z?' z':'')+(o?' tap':'')+'"'+(o?' data-ppi="'+E(i.code)+'"':'')+'><span>'+E(i.name)+'<small>'+E(i.code)+' · '+E(i.unit)+(i.active?'':' · 🙈 hidden from storekeeper')+'</small></span><span class="ppq'+(low||z?' low':'')+'">'+ppN(i.bal)+(z?' <span class="pill p-hi">OUT OF STOCK</span>':'<small> left</small>')+(i.min!==''?'<br><span class="pill '+(low?'p-hi':'p-ok')+'">min '+E(i.min)+'</span>':'')+'</span></div>'});
 el.innerHTML=l.length?h+'</div>':'<p class="msg">Nothing found.</p>'}
function ppTxCard(t,o,early){var x=t.status==='Cancelled',im=t.status==='Imported',ev=[t.apprFile].concat(String(t.ev||'').split(' ')).filter(String);
 return'<div class="ppic'+(x?' x':'')+(t.early?' e':'')+'"><div class="t"><span>'+E(t.ref)+' · '+E(fd(t.date,true))+(t.time?' '+E(t.time):'')+'</span><span>'+(x?'CANCELLED':im?'from Excel':'by '+E(t.by))+'</span></div><b>'+E(t.ecode+' '+t.ename)+(t.site?' · '+E(t.site):'')+'</b><br>'+
  t.lines.map(function(l){return E(l.item)+' ×'+E(ppN(l.qty))+(l.unit?' '+E(l.unit):'')}).join(' · ')+(t.rem?'<br><small>'+E(t.rem)+'</small>':'')+
  (t.early?'<div class="ppe">⏳ EARLY · '+E(t.early)+' · approved by '+E(t.appr)+(t.note?' · '+E(t.note):'')+'</div>':'')+
  (ev.length?'<div class="ppfiles"><button type="button" class="all" data-ppfs="'+E(ev.join(' '))+'" data-ppl="'+E((/Lost/.test(t.early)?'L':'D'))+'" data-ppt2="'+E(t.ref+' · '+t.ecode+' '+t.ename)+'" data-ppo="'+(o?1:0)+'">📷 View '+(/Lost/.test(t.early)?'approval + letter':'approval + damage photos')+' ('+ev.length+')</button></div>':'')+
  (x?'<div class="lock">'+E(t.cxWhy||'')+(t.cxBy?' · by '+E(t.cxBy):'')+'</div>':o&&t.status==='Issued'?'<div style="text-align:right;margin-top:4px"><button type="button" class="ppcx" data-ppx="'+E(t.ref)+'">Cancel…</button></div>':(!o&&!im&&!early)?'<div class="lock">🔒 Mistake? Only the office can cancel – call the office.</div>':'')+'</div>'}
function ppIssList(o){var P=ppP(o),el=$(o?'#ppOList':'#ppList');if(!el)return;var vw=P.role==='view';var q=(($(o?'#ppOIq':'#ppIq')||{}).value||'').trim().toLowerCase();

function ppEmpFind(P,q,max){q=q.trim().toLowerCase();if(!q)return[];return P.emps.filter(function(e){return(e.code+' '+e.name).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0}).slice(0,max||8)}
function ppHFilter(f){return function(e){return!ppHF||(ppHF==='ok'?e.due.some(function(d){return d.ok&&d.last}):e.due.some(function(d){return!d.ok}))}}
function ppHDraw(o){var el=$('#ppHr');if(!el||!ppHist)return;var t=ppHist.today,l=ppHist.list.filter(ppHFilter());

  e.due.filter(function(d){return d.last||PP_ORD.indexOf(d.type)>=0}).map(function(d){return'<div class="pprow"><span style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center">'+ppIco(d.type,'s')+E(ppTN(d.type))+'</span><span style="text-align:right;font-size:11px">'+(d.last?'last '+E(fd(d.last,true))+' · '+E(ppAgo(d.last,t))+'<br>'+E(d.item):'never issued')+'<br>'+(d.ok?'<span class="pill p-ok">✔ Can issue</span>':'<span class="pill p-md">⏳ '+E(fd(d.next,true))+' · early = approval</span>')+'</span></div>'}).join('')+
  (e.rows.length?'<details class="pphd"><summary>History in this period ('+e.rows.length+')</summary>'+e.rows.map(function(r){return'<div class="pphr'+(r.status==='Cancelled'?' x':'')+'"><span>'+E(fd(r.date,true))+'</span><span>'+E(r.item)+(r.early?' <b class="ppeb">EARLY</b>':'')+(r.files?' <button type="button" class="ppfb" data-ppfs="'+E(r.files)+'" data-ppl="'+(/Lost/.test(r.early)?'L':'D')+'" data-ppt2="'+E(r.ref+' · '+e.code+' '+e.name)+'" data-ppo="'+(o?1:0)+'">📷</button>':'')+'</span><span>'+E(ppN(r.qty)+' '+r.unit)+'</span></div>'}).join('')+'</details>':'')+'</div>'}).join(''):'<p class="msg">Nothing for this filter.</p>'}
function ppHArgs(){return{emps:ppHSel.slice(),site:($('#ppHsite')||{}).value||'',from:($('#ppHf')||{}).value||'',to:($('#ppHt')||{}).value||'',show:ppHF}}
async function ppHRun(o,pdf){var f=ppHArgs();if(!f.emps.length&&!f.site){ppMsg(o,'Choose one or more employees (or a site).',true);return}
 var b=document.querySelector('[data-pp="'+(pdf?'hpdf':'hshow')+'"]'),bt=b?b.textContent:'';if(b){b.disabled=true;b.textContent=pdf?'Creating PDF…':'Loading…'}ppMsg(o,'');
 var r;try{r=await run.apply(null,[pdf?'ppePdf':'ppeHistory'].concat(ppWho(o),[f]))}catch(e){r={ok:false,error:'Connection problem. Please try again.'}}
 if(b){b.disabled=false;b.textContent=bt}if(!r.ok){ppMsg(o,E(r.error),true);return}
 if(pdf){pdfRes=r;$('#pdfMsg').textContent=r.name+' · '+r.count+' employee'+(r.count==1?'':'s')+(r.url?' · also saved in your Google Drive folder “WTC Stock PDFs”':' · tap Download to save it on your phone');$('#pdfDrive').hidden=!r.url;if(r.url)$('#pdfDrive').href=r.url;$('#pdfPop').hidden=false;return}
 ppHist=r;ppHDraw(o)}

function ppEarly(){var s=ppIss,P=PPS;if(!s||!s.emp)return[];var L=P.last[s.emp.code]||{},out=[],seen={};

function ppReadFile(f){return new Promise(function(res){if(!f){res('');return}if(/^image\//.test(f.type)){xImg(f,1280,0.72).then(res);return}if(f.size>6e6){res('BIG');return}var rd=new FileReader();rd.onload=function(){res(rd.result)};rd.onerror=function(){res('')};rd.readAsDataURL(f)})}
async function ppGo(){var s=ppIss,P=PPS,m=function(t){$('#ppM').textContent=t};if(!s)return;
 var emp;if(s.isNew){s.nc=(($('#ppNc')||{}).value||'').trim().toUpperCase();s.nn=(($('#ppNn')||{}).value||'').trim();s.site=(($('#ppNs')||{}).value||'').trim();
  if(!s.nc){m('Type the employee code.');return}if(!s.nn){m('Type the full name.');return}var ex=P.emps.filter(function(e){return e.code===s.nc})[0];emp={code:s.nc,name:ex?ex.name:s.nn,site:s.site,isNew:!ex}}
 else if(s.emp){s.site=(($('#ppEs')||{}).value||'').trim();emp={code:s.emp.code,name:s.emp.name,site:s.site,isNew:false}}
 else{m('Choose the employee first.');return}
 if(!emp.site){m('Type the current site of the employee.');return}
 if(!s.lines.length){m('Add at least one PPE item.');return}
 for(var i=0;i<s.lines.length;i++){var it=ppItem(s.lines[i].code),q=Number(String(s.lines[i].qty).replace(',','.'));if(!(q>0)){m('Type the quantity for '+it.name+'.');return}if(it.bal<=0){m(it.name+': 0 in stock. Ask the office to add stock.');return}if(q>it.bal){m('Only '+ppN(it.bal)+' left of '+it.name+'.');return}}
 var ea=ppEarly(),x=s.early;if(ea.length){if(!x.reason){m('Early issue: choose why.');return}if(!x.approved){m('Early issue: tick the approval of '+P.rules.approver+'.');return}if(!x.approval){m('Early issue: add the photo of the approval.');return}if(!x.evidence.length){m(x.reason==='Lost'?'Lost item: add the letter from the Camp Boss.':'Early issue: add at least 1 photo of the damaged item.');return}}
 s.rem=(($('#ppRem')||{}).value||'').trim();x.note=(($('#ppEn')||{}).value||x.note||'').trim();
 var d={emp:emp,lines:s.lines.map(function(l){return{code:l.code,qty:l.qty}}),remarks:s.rem,early:ea.length?{reason:x.reason,approved:x.approved,approval:x.approval,evidence:x.evidence,note:x.note}:null};
 $('#ppPop').hidden=true;showSaving();
 var r;try{r=await run.apply(null,['ppeIssue'].concat(ppWho(false),[d]))}catch(e){r={ok:false,error:'Could not save – photos may be too large or the connection dropped. Please try again.'}}
 if(!r.ok){hideOk();$('#ppPop').hidden=false;m(r.error);return}
 PPS=r.state;ppIss=null;ppTab='iss';ppDraw(false);bigOk(r.early?'PPE issued (early – approved)':'PPE issued',r.msg+' · PPE Excel in Google Drive updated')}

/* ----- events ----- */
document.addEventListener('click',async function(e){var t=e.target,b;
 if(t.id==='ppPop'){ppPopX();return}
 if(b=t.closest('[data-ppg]')){var o=b.dataset.ppo==='1';if(o)ppOTab=b.dataset.ppg;else ppTab=b.dataset.ppg;ppHist=null;ppDraw(o);return}
 if(b=t.closest('[data-pptf]')){ppTF=b.dataset.pptf;var o2=b.dataset.ppo==='1';document.querySelectorAll('#ppTf button').forEach(function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x===b)});ppStockList(o2);return}

var ZV={s:1,x:0,y:0,min:1,p:{},d0:0,s0:1,m0:null,last:0};
function zvApply(){$('#zvI').style.transform='translate('+ZV.x+'px,'+ZV.y+'px) scale('+ZV.s+')';$('#zvP').textContent=Math.round(ZV.s/ZV.min*100)+'%'}
function zvFit(){var w=$('#zvW'),i=$('#zvI'),W=w.clientWidth,H=w.clientHeight,nw=i.naturalWidth||1,nh=i.naturalHeight||1;ZV.min=Math.min(W/nw,H/nh,1);if(ZV.min<=0)ZV.min=1;ZV.s=ZV.min;ZV.x=(W-nw*ZV.s)/2;ZV.y=(H-nh*ZV.s)/2;zvApply()}
function zvZoom(f,cx,cy){var w=$('#zvW');if(cx==null){cx=w.clientWidth/2;cy=w.clientHeight/2}var ns=Math.max(ZV.min,Math.min(ZV.min*10,ZV.s*f));ZV.x=cx-(cx-ZV.x)*ns/ZV.s;ZV.y=cy-(cy-ZV.y)*ns/ZV.s;ZV.s=ns;if(ns===ZV.min){zvFit();return}zvApply()}
function zvOpen(src){if(!src)return;var i=$('#zvI');$('#zv').hidden=false;i.onload=zvFit;i.src=src;if(i.complete)setTimeout(zvFit,0);
 var o=$('#zvO');o.removeAttribute('href');try{if(/^data:/.test(src)){var bin=atob(src.split(',')[1]),u=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(var k=0;k<bin.length;k++)u[k]=bin.charCodeAt(k);o.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([u],{type:(src.match(/^data:([^;]+)/)||[])[1]||'image/jpeg'}))}else o.href=src}catch(e){o.href=src}}
function zvClose(){$('#zv').hidden=true;$('#zvI').src='';ZV.p={}}
document.addEventListener('click',function(e){var im=e.target.closest('#xHero img,#sHero img,.ppgal figure img,.dmbig img');if(im){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();zvOpen(im.src);return}
 var z=e.target.closest('[data-zv]');if(!z)return;var a=z.dataset.zv;if(a==='x')zvClose();else if(a==='in')zvZoom(1.6);else if(a==='out')zvZoom(1/1.6);else if(a==='fit')zvFit()},true);
(function(){var w=document.getElementById('zvW');if(!w)return;
 var pt=function(e){var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();return{x:e.clientX-r.left,y:e.clientY-r.top}};
 w.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){w.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);ZV.p[e.pointerId]=pt(e);var ks=Object.keys(ZV.p);
  if(ks.length===2){var a=ZV.p[ks[0]],b=ZV.p[ks[1]];ZV.d0=Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y);ZV.s0=ZV.s;ZV.m0={x:(a.x+b.x)/2,y:(a.y+b.y)/2,ox:ZV.x,oy:ZV.y}}
  else{var n=Date.now();if(n-ZV.last<300){var q=pt(e);if(ZV.s>ZV.min*1.05)zvFit();else zvZoom(2.5,q.x,q.y)}ZV.last=n}});
 w.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){if(!ZV.p[e.pointerId])return;var q=pt(e),o=ZV.p[e.pointerId],ks=Object.keys(ZV.p);ZV.p[e.pointerId]=q;
  if(ks.length===2&&ZV.m0){var a=ZV.p[ks[0]],b=ZV.p[ks[1]],d=Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y),ns=Math.max(ZV.min,Math.min(ZV.min*10,ZV.s0*d/(ZV.d0||1))),m={x:(a.x+b.x)/2,y:(a.y+b.y)/2};
   ZV.x=m.x-(ZV.m0.x-ZV.m0.ox)*ns/ZV.s0;ZV.y=m.y-(ZV.m0.y-ZV.m0.oy)*ns/ZV.s0;ZV.s=ns;zvApply()}
  else if(ks.length===1&&ZV.s>ZV.min*1.01){ZV.x+=q.x-o.x;ZV.y+=q.y-o.y;zvApply()}});
 var up=function(e){delete ZV.p[e.pointerId];if(Object.keys(ZV.p).length<2)ZV.m0=null;if(ZV.s<=ZV.min*1.01&&!Object.keys(ZV.p).length)zvFit()};
 w.addEventListener('pointerup',up);w.addEventListener('pointercancel',up);
 w.addEventListener('wheel',function(e){e.preventDefault();var q=pt(e);zvZoom(e.deltaY<0?1.15:1/1.15,q.x,q.y)},{passive:false});
 window.addEventListener('resize',function(){if(!$('#zv').hidden)zvFit()});
 document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape'&&!$('#zv').hidden)zvClose()})})();

 b.className='wkb'+(late?' late':urgent?' warn':'');
