/* ---------- site services: waste skip changed · water tank filled · septic tank emptied · diesel / petrol bought ---------- */
var SVK={skip:{ic:'♻️',btn:'Skips changed',verb:'Changed',c:'#4E7D2B',bg:'#EAF3E3',q:'How many full skips did the supplier change?',sub:'full taken away · empty placed',unit:'skip'},
 water:{ic:'💧',btn:'Tank filled',verb:'Filled',c:'#1F6FB2',bg:'#E4F0FA',q:'How many tanks did the water tanker fill?',sub:'filled to full',unit:'tank'},
 septic:{ic:'🚛',btn:'Tank emptied',verb:'Emptied',c:'#8A5A12',bg:'#F7EEDD',q:'How many tanks did the sewage tanker empty?',sub:'emptied by the supplier',unit:'tank'},
 fuel:{ic:'⛽',btn:'Fuel bought',verb:'Bought',c:'#B5480F',bg:'#FBEDE3',unit:'litre'}};
var MN3=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'],svId=null,svWhere='',svInto='';
function svcKind(n){n=String(n||'').trim().toUpperCase();return /^WASTE SKIP/.test(n)?'skip':/^WATER TANK/.test(n)?'water':/^GRP SEPTIC TANK/.test(n)?'septic':(n==='DIESEL'||n==='PETROL')?'fuel':''}
function svN(x,dp){return Number(x).toLocaleString('en-US',{minimumFractionDigits:dp||0,maximumFractionDigits:dp||0})}
function svLine(k,v,mon){var K=SVK[k],m=MN3[+mon-1];if(!v||!v[3])return K.ic+' '+K.verb+' in '+m+': none yet';
 return K.ic+' '+K.verb+' in '+m+': '+(k==='fuel'?svN(v[0])+' L'+(v[1]?' · AED '+svN(v[1]):''):svN(v[0]))+' · last '+fd(v[2])}
function svcRows(ro){var s=(cur&&cur.svc)||{},mon=String((cur&&cur.today)||today).slice(5,7);
 document.querySelectorAll('#list .it[data-svk]').forEach(function(row){var id=row.dataset.id,k=row.dataset.svk,l=row.querySelector('.svl');
  l.hidden=!!ro;l.textContent=svLine(k,s[id],mon);row.querySelectorAll('.svb,.svh').forEach(function(b){b.hidden=!!ro})})}
function svPopOpen(id){var it=imap[id];if(!it)return;var k=svcKind(it[1]),K=SVK[k],src=imgs[it[5]],have=base(id)||'0',t=(cur&&cur.today)||today;svId=id;svWhere='';svInto='';
 var head='<h4 style="color:'+K.c+'">'+K.ic+' '+K.btn.toUpperCase()+'</h4><div class="svt">'+(src?'<img alt="" src="'+src+'">':'')+'<span>'+E(it[1])+'<small>'+E($('#site').value)+' · '+E($('#name').value)+'</small></span></div>';
 var h;
 if(k==='fuel'){
  h=head+'<div class="svnow" style="background:'+K.bg+'"><span>In stock now</span><b style="color:'+K.c+'">'+E(have)+' L</b></div>'+
   '<label for="svQ">1. How many litres were bought?</label><div class="svbig" style="border-color:'+K.c+'"><input id="svQ" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" placeholder="0"><span>litres</span></div>'+
   '<div class="r2"><div><label for="svD">2. Date</label><input type="date" id="svD" value="'+t+'" max="'+t+'"></div><div><label for="svA">3. Amount AED (optional)</label><input id="svA" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" placeholder="0.00"></div></div>'+
   '<label for="svS">4. Fuel station / supplier</label><input id="svS" maxlength="80" placeholder="e.g. fuel station name or diesel supplier">'+
   '<label for="svT">5. Receipt / LPO no. (optional)</label><input id="svT" maxlength="60">'+
   '<label>6. Where did it go?</label><div class="svopt" data-w="tank"><i>🛢️</i><span><b>Into our fuel tank / cans</b><small id="svTk">Added to the stock of '+E(it[1].toLowerCase())+'</small></span></div>'+
   '<div class="svopt" data-w="direct"><i>🚜</i><span><b>Straight into machines / vehicles</b><small>Not added to stock – recorded as bought and used</small><div class="svinto" hidden><div class="svch"><button data-i="Generator">Generator</button><button data-i="Machine">Machine</button><button data-i="Vehicle">Vehicle</button></div><input id="svI" maxlength="80" placeholder="Which one? (optional) e.g. Generator 250 kVA"></div></span></div>'+
   '<div class="svres" id="svR" hidden></div>';
 }else{
  h=head+'<div class="svnow" style="background:'+K.bg+'"><span>On site now<small>(the Total of this item)</small></span><b style="color:'+K.c+'">'+E(have)+'</b></div>'+
   '<label for="svQ">'+K.q+'</label><div class="svstp" style="border-color:'+K.c+'"><button data-s="-1" style="color:'+K.c+'">−</button><input id="svQ" inputmode="numeric" value="1" autocomplete="off"><button data-s="1" style="color:'+K.c+'">+</button></div><span class="msg" style="margin-left:8px">'+K.sub+'</span>'+
   '<label for="svD">Date</label><input type="date" id="svD" value="'+t+'" max="'+t+'">'+
   '<label for="svT">Supplier ticket / delivery note no. (optional)</label><input id="svT" maxlength="60">'+
   '<div class="svnote">This does <b>not</b> change the number of '+(k==='skip'?'skips':'tanks')+' on site. It only counts how many times the supplier '+(k==='skip'?'changed':k==='water'?'filled':'emptied')+' them.</div>';
 }
 h+='<p class="msg" id="svM" style="color:#B8274A;font-weight:600"></p><div class="svbtns"><button class="c" id="svC">Cancel</button><button id="svGo" style="background:'+K.c+'">Save</button></div>';

function svUI(){var k=$('#svPop').dataset.k,K=SVK[k],q=Number(String(($('#svQ')||{}).value||'').replace(/,/g,''))||0,b=$('#svGo');if(!b)return;
 if(k==='fuel'){var a=Number(String($('#svA').value||'').replace(/,/g,''))||0,have=Number(base(svId)||0),r=$('#svR');
  document.querySelectorAll('#svPopC .svopt').forEach(function(o){o.classList.toggle('on',o.dataset.w===svWhere);o.style.borderColor=o.dataset.w===svWhere?K.c:''});

var AI={hist:[],busy:false,rec:null,el:null,ctx:null,src:null,n:0};
var AI_TIPS=['Which sites have not done the weekly check?','What PPE is low or out of stock?','How much cement is at each site?','Any late tasks or overdue rentals?','What was written off as damaged this week?','Which excess materials are in the store?'];

/* phones only let a page play sound that starts from a tap – so the first tap opens the sound for later */
function aiUnlock(){try{if(!AI.ctx){var C=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(C)AI.ctx=new C()}if(AI.ctx&&AI.ctx.state==='suspended')AI.ctx.resume();if(AI.ctx){var b=AI.ctx.createBuffer(1,1,22050),s=AI.ctx.createBufferSource();s.buffer=b;s.connect(AI.ctx.destination);s.start(0)}}catch(e){}
 try{if(window.speechSynthesis&&!AI.sp){AI.sp=1;var u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(' ');u.volume=0;speechSynthesis.speak(u)}}catch(e){}}
function aiQuiet(){try{if(AI.src){AI.src.onended=null;AI.src.stop()}}catch(e){}AI.src=null;try{if(window.speechSynthesis)speechSynthesis.cancel()}catch(e){}try{if(AI.rec)AI.rec.abort()}catch(e){}AI.rec=null;$('#aiMic').classList.remove('on');AI.n++;if(!AI.busy)aiSt('')}
function aiPcmWav(bytes,rate){var h=new ArrayBuffer(44),v=new DataView(h),w=function(o,s){for(var i=0;i<s.length;i++)v.setUint8(o+i,s.charCodeAt(i))};
 w(0,'RIFF');v.setUint32(4,36+bytes.length,true);w(8,'WAVE');w(12,'fmt ');v.setUint32(16,16,true);v.setUint16(20,1,true);v.setUint16(22,1,true);v.setUint32(24,rate,true);v.setUint32(28,rate*2,true);v.setUint16(32,2,true);v.setUint16(34,16,true);w(36,'data');v.setUint32(40,bytes.length,true);
 var o=new Uint8Array(44+bytes.length);o.set(new Uint8Array(h),0);o.set(bytes,44);return o}
function aiPlay(b64,mime,n){return new Promise(function(res,rej){try{
 var bin=atob(b64),u=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(var i=0;i<bin.length;i++)u[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
 if(!/wav/i.test(mime||'')){var m=/rate=(\d+)/.exec(mime||'');u=aiPcmWav(u,m?+m[1]:24000)}
 if(!AI.ctx)return rej('no audio');AI.ctx.resume();
 AI.ctx.decodeAudioData(u.buffer,function(buf){if(n!==AI.n)return res();var s=AI.ctx.createBufferSource();s.buffer=buf;s.connect(AI.ctx.destination);s.onended=function(){if(AI.src===s){AI.src=null;aiSt('')}res()};AI.src=s;s.start(0)},function(){rej('decode')})}catch(e){rej(e)}})}
function aiPhoneVoice(text,n){return new Promise(function(res){if(!window.speechSynthesis||n!==AI.n)return res(false);
 var lang=$('#aiLang').value,want=lang.slice(0,2),vs=speechSynthesis.getVoices().filter(function(v){return v.lang&&v.lang.slice(0,2).toLowerCase()===want});
 if(/[؀-ۿ]/.test(text)&&want!=='ar')vs=speechSynthesis.getVoices().filter(function(v){return/^(ur|hi)/i.test(v.lang)});
 if(/[ঀ-৿]/.test(text))vs=speechSynthesis.getVoices().filter(function(v){return/^bn/i.test(v.lang)});
 var score=function(v){var s=v.name;return(/natural|neural|premium|enhanced|siri/i.test(s)?4:0)+(/google/i.test(s)?3:0)+(/samantha|daniel|serena|libby|sonia|aria|jenny/i.test(s)?2:0)+(v.localService?0:1)};
 vs.sort(function(a,b){return score(b)-score(a)});
 var u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);if(vs[0]){u.voice=vs[0];u.lang=vs[0].lang}else u.lang=lang;u.rate=0.97;u.onend=u.onerror=function(){aiSt('');res(true)};speechSynthesis.cancel();speechSynthesis.speak(u)})}
async function aiSpeak(text){var v=$('#aiVoice').value,n=++AI.n;if(v==='off'||!text)return;
 if(v!=='phone'){aiSt('Getting the voice ready',true);var r=null;try{r=await run('aiVoice',$('#pin').value.trim(),text,v)}catch(e){}
  if(n!==AI.n)return;
  if(r&&r.ok){aiSt('Speaking',true);try{await aiPlay(r.data,r.mime,n);return}catch(e){}}}
 if(n!==AI.n)return;aiSt('Speaking',true);await aiPhoneVoice(text,n)}
async function aiGo(q){q=String(q||'').trim();if(!q||AI.busy)return;aiQuiet();aiUnlock();$('#aiQ').value='';
 aiAdd('aiMe',E(q));AI.busy=true;aiSt('Checking your data');keepAct();
 var r;try{r=await run('aiAsk',$('#pin').value.trim(),q,AI.hist)}catch(e){r={ok:false,error:'Connection problem. Please try again.'}}
 AI.busy=false;aiSt('');
 if(!r||!r.ok){aiAdd('aiBot aiErr','⚠ '+E((r&&r.error)||'Something went wrong.'));return}
 AI.hist.push({q:q,a:r.say});if(AI.hist.length>6)AI.hist.shift();
 var d=aiAdd('aiBot','<div>'+E(r.say)+'</div>'+(r.show&&r.show.length?'<ul>'+r.show.map(function(s){return'<li>'+E(s)+'</li>'}).join('')+'</ul>':'')+'<div class="aiAct"><button type="button" data-aisay>🔊 Play again</button></div>');
 d.querySelector('[data-aisay]').onclick=function(){aiQuiet();aiUnlock();aiSpeak(r.say)};
 aiSpeak(r.say)}
function keepAct(){try{lastAct=Date.now()}catch(e){}}
function aiKb(){var q=$('#aiQ');q.focus();var h=function(){$('#aiSt').innerHTML='<span>⌨️ Tap the <b>🎤</b> on your keyboard and speak, then tap ➤</span>'};h();setTimeout(h,80)}
function aiListen(){aiQuiet();aiUnlock();var SR=window.SpeechRecognition||window.webkitSpeechRecognition;if(aiGet('nomic','')){aiKb();return}
 if(!SR){aiAdd('aiBot aiErr','🎙 Voice typing is not available in this browser. Tap the box and use the 🎤 key on your keyboard instead.');$('#aiQ').focus();return}
 var r=new SR(),got='';AI.rec=r;r.lang=$('#aiLang').value;r.interimResults=true;r.continuous=false;r.maxAlternatives=1;
 r.onstart=function(){$('#aiMic').classList.add('on');aiSt('Listening… speak now',true)};
 r.onresult=function(e){var t='',fin=false;for(var i=0;i<e.results.length;i++){t+=e.results[i][0].transcript;if(e.results[i].isFinal)fin=true}$('#aiQ').value=t;got=t;if(fin){got=t}};
 r.onerror=function(e){$('#aiMic').classList.remove('on');aiSt('');if(e.error==='not-allowed'||e.error==='service-not-allowed'||e.error==='audio-capture'){aiPut('nomic','1');aiKb()}else if(e.error==='no-speech')aiSt('');};
 r.onend=function(){$('#aiMic').classList.remove('on');if(AI.rec===r){AI.rec=null;aiSt('');if(got.trim())aiGo(got)}};
 try{r.start()}catch(e){aiSt('')}}
$('#aiFab').onclick=aiOpen;$('#aiX').onclick=function(){aiQuiet();$('#aiPop').hidden=true};
$('#aiPop').onclick=function(e){if(e.target===this){aiQuiet();this.hidden=true}};
$('#aiNew').onclick=function(){aiQuiet();AI.hist=[];aiHello()};
$('#aiMic').onclick=function(){if(AI.rec){var r=AI.rec;try{r.stop()}catch(e){}return}aiListen()};
$('#aiSend').onclick=function(){aiGo($('#aiQ').value)};
$('#aiQ').onkeydown=function(e){if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();aiGo(this.value)}};
$('#aiLog').onclick=function(e){var b=e.target.closest('[data-aitip]');if(b)aiGo(b.dataset.aitip)};
$('#aiSt').onclick=function(e){if(e.target.id==='aiStop')aiQuiet()};
$('#aiLang').value=aiGet('lang','en-GB');$('#aiVoice').value=aiGet('voice','Kore');
$('#aiLang').onchange=function(){aiPut('lang',this.value)};$('#aiVoice').onchange=function(){aiPut('voice',this.value);aiQuiet()};
try{if(window.speechSynthesis)speechSynthesis.getVoices()}catch(e){}
</script></body></html>
